(3) The boards' selection counts ("3 by 2 tiles selected", "Tile (4, 4) selected", GizmoStates' "1 selected") stand at the right before the area's counts: built; the old line's one-selected readout (kind, tag, blueprint, place and facing) is "1 selected"'s tip. (4) Main draws Q and E as two caps with nothing between, AreaPaint as Q / E: Turn by a step as Main, a tool's Turn as AreaPaint. (5) GizmoStates' Shift held draws the held key in `accent-fill` (`.kbd.held`) and the handles as glyphs: built (`HintPart::Held`, `marks::paint_held_key_cap`; the turning ring, axes, tilt rings and scale handle drawn in the handles' own colors), Tilt and Scale only where the selection's models take a visual transform (a static placeable's, a waypoint's do not). (6) The plan's "Esc and Enter ... for a dialog in front per T11": no board draws a dialog's keys in the Status Bar (Dialogs and Pickers have none; Wizard keeps the area's hints under the Area Wizard), so a dialog shows its Close's "(Esc)" tip as T11 built, and the Status Bar keeps the active editor's hints. (7) The Options board's hints (Enter OK, Tab Next field, Shift + Tab Previous field) gain Esc Cancel (R1); Enter OK only while the pointer is over Options and nothing has the keys, where its Enter is OK (`options::ui`); a Properties tab has no Enter (its OK reads none): Esc, Tab, Shift + Tab. (8) The plan's `.sb-log` caps: 96 beside an area (Main, AreaPaint, AreaTiles) and 160 beside any other tab (the Creature board's, within the 150 to 260 of the others). (9) The Resources board's hints, which the moves table gave to T12: built for the Resources panel in use (Up or Down Move in the list and Enter Open where its list has the keys, Double Click Open, Right Click More, Find's key with the pointer over it); its count's "double-click to open" tip, kept for T12, is gone. (10) Other boards' hints: the Script board's keys were cheap (each a keymap command) and are built; the rest are M4's (below). **Built.** The kit (`mg-uikit`; what existed from T1: `Hint`, `Hint::keys`, `either`, `key`, `plus`, `or`, `part`, `HintPart` with key caps, joiners, icons and glyphs, `Hints::new`, `show`, `fitting`, `hints_that_fit`, the hints' metrics): added `Hint::note`, `is_note`, `held`, `parts`, `HintPart::Held`, `Hints::width`, `status::NOTE_LEAST`, `marks::paint_held_key_cap`; changed, the kit being in no tag: `Hint::spoken` says "Shift, Right Drag: Turn" ("Q or E: Turn", a note its words; it said "Shift + Right Drag Turn"), a note has no margin before its word, `Hints::show` measures each hint once and draws a note cut short, `Hints::fitting` counts that note. `mg-ui`: `shell::shown` (what the bar shows for the active docked editor: an area's state, `area_view::bar::status`; the Welcome page's New module, Open module and Quick Open; a script's eight keys; a tab with OK and Cancel; the Resources panel in use over any), `shell::HintKeys` (a command's first key as bound, `keys::caps`: modifiers by the system's names, Cmd and Option on macOS, keys as the boards spell them, Esc, Page Up; an unbound command's hint left out), `Shown`, `Count`, `last_line_cap`; `status_bar` lays out the middle (a separator, the line held to its cap, the hints in what it leaves) and the counts before the job; `area_view::bar` (the states, below; the glyphs); `paint_mode::hints` (for `status`), `terrain_mode::spot_text` (for `status`); `tabs::Viewer::foresee_escape` (T10's rule, foreseen where nothing has the keys and no list drawn before the editor area, the module tree, the Resources panel or the log, holds them; an Escape that would be another tab's than the active editor's comes first, "Esc: Cancel Module Properties"; Options' Enter not while its Keyboard page waits for a key); the hint line under the area toolbar and its counts are gone (the view has their height), the toolbar keeping "problems" and "N missing" (warnings of what was read, beside its tools). The bar is drawn before the editor area, so what it shows is made once a frame after the editor area (`shell::update_shown`, since the review below; before it, `shown` ran twice a frame, at the bar and after the editor area) and drawn by the next frame's bar, a frame asked for where it changed, which idle windows never do. Public: `Moonglow::status_said` (what the bar says), `Moonglow::status_for_tests` (hidden). **The states** (each with its notes first): nothing selected; selected (Drag Move, the ring Turn, Shift Handles, or Alt + Right Drag Turn with the ring off; Alt + Drag Raise, Q E Turn by a step, G Drop to ground); Shift held (Shift held, Move along an axis, Tilt, Scale, Right Drag Turn view); a drag (Esc Cancel, Middle Drag Turn view or Alt + Middle Drag with Options' swap, the snap's grid or angle); a box (Esc Cancel); read-only (Click Select and the view's); placing a blueprint (by kind: an outline's corners, a door's, others with Q / E and Page Up / Page Down), the start location, a light, an appearance; pasting objects; pasting tiles (new: the line had none); Paint (nothing chosen, painting, erasing, filling, a row; "N more are laid than are drawn"); a tile brush (each brush, over an object "a click or drag here goes to the object", Exact Tile, the refusal; "In hand: …", the tile under the pointer at the right); Redraw Polygon; Ctrl + wheel's scale (Scale 1.25, Ctrl + Wheel, let go of Ctrl to keep, Esc Drop); a refused scale (its note and Ctrl + Wheel Zoom slowly, four seconds, a frame asked for at their end); Select Tiles (its notice, the AreaTiles board's six and Shift + Drag Add a box). **The yield order** (one, in `status_bar`'s comment, the manual and here): the game folder goes first; then the hints, whole, from the end; then the last line, from its cap down to 48 points, then gone; then the counts, from the end; then the job's name is cut short; last the module's name. **What a builder notices.** The line under the area toolbar is gone and the view is a line taller; what it said is in the Status Bar as key caps, its notes at their head, its counts and the selection at the right; the keys shown are the builder's own; the Welcome page and the Resources panel say their keys; a docked Options or Properties tab says Esc where Escape would cancel it. **Tests.** Kit: `hints_lay_out_with_and_without_the_theme`, `a_notice_without_room_is_cut_short`, `a_hint_says_its_keys_and_word` (new form), `hints_drop_whole_at_a_narrow_width` (names migrated); unit `a_shortcut_s_caps_follow_the_system`. App, `tests/app/status_hints.rs` (new): `each_area_state_shows_its_hints`, `a_read_only_area_hints_no_change`, `a_rebound_key_shows_as_bound_and_an_unbound_one_drops_its_hint`, `the_welcome_page_shows_its_keys_as_bound`, `the_counts_follow_the_active_editor[_as_tabs]`, `the_resources_panel_in_use_shows_its_hints`, `a_script_tab_shows_its_keys`, and the hand-run `hints_cost`; `open_editors.rs`: `the_esc_hint_shows_where_the_escape_is_the_tab_s[_as_tabs]`, `the_esc_hint_names_another_tab_than_the_active_editor[_as_tabs]`, `docked_options_hints_enter_where_it_is_ok`; `chrome_colors.rs` keeps the glyphs' six handle colors (the 3D view's, not the theme's); `status_bar.rs`'s sweep now has the Main board's six hints and two counts, from 420 to 1600 by 8, to 2560 by 16 and 3440, asserting the order and each stage seen. Migrated: `tiles.rs` (`status_line` reads the bar; the brush's note; the terrain's), `options_and_keys.rs` (New's tip apart from its hint), `status_bar.rs` (the line's cap; T7's line tests with no hints), `common::tip_at` (passes the Status Bar by). **Pictures** (`target/ui-mock/impl/m3-t12/`, before, dark, light): `shell_main`, `shell_wide`, `shell_welcome`, `shell_narrow`, `shell_status*`, `area-paint*`, `area-toolbar-narrow*`, `shell_area_states` (new: eleven states, `status_bars_area_states.png` their bars) and `shell_status_hints` (new: an area's hints and counts at 420, 640, 1000 and 1600). **Timing.** `frame_perf` and `editor_perf` (release, `target/ui-mock/perf-m3/t12/`) against the tip's run before T12 (`perf-m3/m3b/`): all pass; over budget only the known area084 overview and turning under the wheel (8.4 to 10.0 ms, before 8.8 to 10.1) and area092's edit frame 7.3 ms (7.6 before). Two runs: the first at a 1-minute load of 1.4 falling to 0.7 put most area views 0.3 to 1.2 ms slower; the second, at 0.8 to 1.0, most of the same views 0.3 to 1.0 ms faster (area084 close and turning 0.7 to 0.9 faster, area092 all selected with the walkmesh 0.6 to 0.8 faster) and lake's with Moonglow's shader 0.3 to 0.6 slower: the runs' own spread, wider than any difference between the trees (`frame_perf_run1.log`, `frame_perf.log`, `loads.txt`). `editor_perf`: two views moved by more than 0.3 ms, both faster (a table's Find with a cell set each frame 3.12 to 2.54 ms, Select Texture's click opened again 1.76 to 1.40). While the machine was loaded by other sessions (1-minute load 7 to 20), also back to back three times on this tree and on its parent (`git archive HEAD`), a release frame of the whole 1600 by 900 window with an 8 by 8 area in front (`target/ui-mock/perf-m3/t12/t12_cost.log`, loads 14.7, 13.7, 13.7): nothing selected 0.700, 0.683, 0.681 ms against the parent's 0.684, 0.699, 0.695; one selected 0.734, 0.734, 0.717 against 0.717, 0.720, 0.723; Select Tiles 0.705, 0.716, 0.710 against 0.710, 0.706, 0.712: no difference beyond the runs' own spread. The hand-run `hints_cost` (release, load 9.5): a frame with the fullest hint set (Select Tiles' seven and a notice, three counts) 0.078 ms against 0.062 with none, 0.016 ms for the hints; no cache of their own beyond measuring each once a frame (`MeasuredHints`, since the review below: the bar measured each three times, for its width, its fit and its drawing; egui's galley cache keeps their words from frame to frame), as `LookCache` caches tab titles: none needed at that cost. **Inherits:** T13: the Outliner's rows may bring hints of their own (click selects, the eye) through `shell::shown`. T14: under W 1400 the switches become icons, which the order above assumes beside it; the folder drops first already. M4 (each editor's change): the Creature and WideCreature boards' 3D view hints (Drag Turn view, Right Drag Move view, Middle Drag, Wheel, Shift + Wheel) to the blueprints; the script's "Line 17, column 12" count and the Script board's 170 cap to the script editor; the Conversation board's hints and "3834 words" to the conversation editor; the Table and Talk boards' hints and counts ("6 cells", "840 rows, 59 columns", "112228 lines") to the tables; NUI, Tileset, Hak ("2706 resources, 183.8 MB", "3 selected"), Journal and Factions boards' to the rest; GalleryPalette's and AppearanceGallery's (and "838 appearances") to the galleries; the Resources board's counts at the bar's right (its count stays in the panel) to the rest; the gizmos' readout chips beside the drag hints to the gizmos; the viewer's boards to M6.

**Review, 2026-11-13.** An independent review of T12 (its commit `148485e2`) found, and this round fixed: (1) **Enter: OK shown where Enter does something else**: with the module tree holding the keys (a press on the Left Dock, F6) and the pointer over docked Options, the bar said Enter: OK while the tree takes Enter first (`tree.rs`'s `consume_key`). Enter's hint now takes the same exclusion as the Esc hint, `palette_view::a_list_has_the_keys` (the module tree, the Resources panel, the log); `docked_options_hints_enter_where_it_is_ok` holds it (the tree's keys, nothing focused: no Enter, and Enter leaves Options open); broken, "never: no Enter, the tree's keys". (2) **Two Esc guards whose tests could not fail**: the "menu open" step clicked View, so the pointer and the last press left the tab and the hint went for that reason. `esc_hint` now opens the tab's own menu (a right click on its tab, nothing focused, the pointer over its body; Escape closes the menu, the tab stays) and holds the module tree's keys with nothing focused (asserted); broken alone, the popup guard gives "never: no Esc hint, the tab's menu open" and the list guard "never: no Esc hint, the tree's keys" (both modes). The review saw the list guard pass when broken because the tree's keys came with egui's focus; in this tree after F6 nothing has the focus (now asserted), and the break failed at the commit too: the test's press on the Left Dock before F6 is what lets the pointer's rule reach the tab. A dropdown of Options open is no case of the popup guard alone: it takes the keyboard (egui's focus), so neither Esc nor Enter is hinted for that reason, which the Options test holds. (3) **A floating model's window under the pointer**: an Escape closes it (`tabs::Viewer::body`'s `model`, no check of the docked tab seen), so the docked tab the keyboard is in is not canceled; the hint now goes where the pointer is over such a window (`Moonglow::model_under_pointer_next`, set where `body` computes `model`). Of the windows' paths, it is the one that takes Escape whatever a docked tab saw: a window in front with OK and Cancel takes it only where no docked tab did, dialogs and menus are the guards already there. `a_model_window_under_the_pointer_takes_the_esc_hint[_as_tabs]`: the last press in Module Properties, the pointer over a model's window, no hint; Escape closes the window, the tab stays; broken, "never: no Esc hint, the model's window under the pointer". (4) **The area's hints while they cannot act**: the view's keys and presses need `response.hovered()`; a modal (a job's window, `egui::Modal`), a dialog (Resize Area) and an open menu each kept Q from turning the selection with the pointer over the view (measured), so `shell::keys_blocked` (any of the three) clears the area's hints, the counts kept. With the pointer elsewhere in the window the hints stay (they say what the view's keys do); the manual (02, 04) says the area's keys act with the pointer over the view. `nothing_in_front_or_no_area_hints` (menu, dialog, modal; back after each); broken, "never: no hints, a menu open: [\"Drag: Move\", …]", and without the modal, "never: no hints, a modal in front". (5) **The readout during a drag**: the old line read the selected object from the area's model, not the posed one a drag moves (`AreaView::posed`), so it showed where the object was before the drag, not live; it was visible at rest, though, which "1 selected"'s tip is not. Now while one object is dragged or turned (a drag, or Q and E's turning) its kind, tag, blueprint, place and facing lead the hints as a note, read from the posed model as it moves; at rest it stays "1 selected"'s tip, as GizmoStates draws "1 selected" (its mid-drag hints are Esc, Middle Drag and the snap, and its chips on the view are M4's gizmos'). A note at the hints' head, not an item at the right: the readout changes each frame of a drag, which the right-hand items do not, and the GizmoStates board's mid-drag notes ("Snap 0.5 m") are in the hints. `the_object_moved_reads_where_it_is`; broken, "the place read as it moves: []", read from the model "…: [\"Waypoint NW_WAYPOINT001 (nw_waypoint001) at 20.00, 20.00, 0.00, facing 107°\"]", and without Q's "read while Q turns it". (6) **Smaller untruths**: the ring's Turn and Q, E Turn by a step now show only where something selected turns (`turns`: not a sound), Alt + Drag Raise only where something selected is not a creature (they stand on the ground). With the ring on, Alt + Right Drag still turns, but the Main board draws the ring alone for a selection (Main-status draws Alt + Right Drag for nothing selected, where it does nothing): the ring alone is hinted, decided by the board. Broken, `the_other_area_states_show_their_hints` gives "never said [\"Drag: Move\", \"Alt, Drag: Raise\"] without [\"Turning ring: Turn\", …]" and "never said [\"Turning ring: Turn\", …] without [\"Alt, Drag: Raise\"]". (7) **The cost claim**: each hint was measured three times a frame and `shown` made twice; now the kit's `Hints::measure` gives `MeasuredHints` (width, fit, drawing from one measure) and `shown` is made once a frame after the editor area (`shell::update_shown`; `Moonglow::status_said` returns what it made). Measured (`shown_cost_on_a_large_area`, release, `perf-m3/t12/review_cost.log`, load about 7): on 549 objects (area084's count) the making takes 0.0011 ms with nothing selected, 0.0020 with all 549 selected, 0.0015 with Shift held; the fullest hint set's frame 0.074 ms against 0.062 without (`hints_cost`). (8) **Tests and pictures**: `the_other_area_states_show_their_hints` covers a sound alone, a creature alone (and Shift: Tilt, Scale), Ctrl + wheel's scale, a static placeable's refusal for its four seconds (the window's own time, 3 to 5 s), pasting objects and tiles, Redraw Polygon, placing an appearance, a trigger and a door, a tile brush over an object and Select Tiles' notice first; broken, the refusal never going and the brush's note gone each fail it. The Resources test's last step asserts Module Properties' hints by name. The Light pictures now include `shell_welcome`, `shell_wide`, `shell_status_hints_*` and `status_bars_area_states`. Also: `tiles::a_brush_s_tiles_are_drawn_by_the_game_s_shaders` waited 40 frames for the game's programs and failed once under load; it waits with a deadline (half a minute) for them to be made, and, the programs withheld (Moonglow's shader), fails "the area's tiles, by the game's programs". `area_view.rs`'s `area_viewer_places_draws_boxes_and_turns` read the bar's "Placing nw_waypoint001" one frame after choosing the blueprint; the bar now shows it a frame later still, so it waits for it with a deadline. Disagreed with and said: the readout was never live (finding 5's wording); the list guard's test did fail when broken at the commit (finding 2), though it is now independent of focus by assertion.

**T13. The Outliner (R2, ruled).**

- **Goal.**
  - The Left Dock splits: Module/Resources over the **Outliner** (resizable seam).
  - For an area: the placed objects by kind, each with its count and an **eye** bound to `view.show[kind]` (the same state as the area toolbar's Show toggles); rows with a kind icon, name and weak tag; click selects in the view; the selected object is revealed.
  - Shown only while the active editor brings one (the area in M3).
  - **Inherits from T6:** the rule is built (`Moonglow::active_editor`, `shell::editor_brings_palette`); the Outliner's `Panel::available` takes the same test (an area's tab or the placeholder), and a Left Dock whose panels none is available is not drawn (the Module and Resources panels keep it while a module is open).
- **Files.** New `outliner.rs` (reuse the tree's per-area contents code, `tree.rs` contents and row helpers), `shell.rs`.
- **Tests.**
  - App: the eye hides a kind in the view and toggles the area toolbar's "Show Creatures"; selecting in the view marks the row and selecting a row selects in the view.
  - Accessibility: the group is named "Outliner", so `area_tool` and Outliner eyes do not collide.
  - Picture against Main.png and LightArea_crop_outl.png.
- **Risks.**
  - **`frame_perf` must run**: area084's 549 objects; kinds closed by default except the selection's; `show_rows`.
  - The module tree keeps its opened-out area rows until R2 is answered.
- **Manual.** 02 (new Outliner section), 04-areas.

**Done, 2026-11-14.** **Plan against board** (the boards won where they differ): (1) The plan's "rows with a kind icon, name and weak tag": the boards draw the kind's icon on the kind's row alone; an object's row is its name and its tag weak after it, indented, no icon: built so. (2) "Shown only while the active editor brings one (an area's tab or the placeholder)": the placeholder ("No area open") holds nothing to list and the plan's own rule (no merge point shows an empty panel, S5) and no board draw an Outliner beside it: the Outliner comes with an area's tab alone (`shell::editor_brings_outliner`, as `editor_brings_palette` reads `active_editor`, but without needing the game's data). (3) The kinds: nine (Creatures to Waypoints, the GIT's order, the toolbar's), no Start row (no board draws one); a kind with nothing placed is a full row with its count 0 and its eye and no arrow (Items 0 on the board), not quiet; quiet (`text-3`) means hidden in the view (Encounters, Sounds, Triggers, Waypoints on the board), where the tree's quiet empty kind is a label. (4) The header: "Outliner", the area's name weak after it, Expand All and Collapse All (the boards' two buttons), the kit's `PanelHeader`. (5) The seam: Main.html's `.module-panel` is 496 high (Foundations' 3:2 is the overview's), kept as the board's split until dragged. (6) The board's eye names flip with the state ("Hide creatures", "Show encounters"); built as a toggle named "Show Creatures" said pressed or not (a toggle named for what it does when on, as the area toolbar's; a name that changes with the state is not one a screen reader or a test can find again), its tip "Show or hide the creatures in the area's view". (7) Narrow.png's strip has a third icon: the Outliner's, built. (8) No board draws Status Bar hints for the Outliner: none built (T12's sentence said "may"); its Enter and Escape count in T12's rule (`a_list_has_the_keys`). (9) Objects are listed in the module tree's order (by the tree's row text: a waypoint by its tag), each row saying its name and tag; the board's rows are a sample of four. (10) The selection's row is brought to the middle of the Outliner when out of sight (the tree's rule): with 123 placeables the kinds above scroll out, where the board shows a short list under its kind. (11) AreaPaint, AreaTiles and Floating draw the same Outliner as Main; AreaTiles keeps Placeables open with no row marked (Select Tiles, no object selected): the kinds keep their open state, as built. **Built.** Kit (`mg-uikit`, added): `TreeGroup::quiet`, `TreeGroup::eye`, `TreeGroup::show_eyed`, `TreeRow::quiet`, `TreeRow::second` (a weak second word, the two cut with an ellipsis to the row's width, the name to at most 70%), `TreeRow::fit`, `TreeRowShown::eye`, `tree::EYE`, `EYE_ICON`, `SUB_GAP`, `NAME_SHARE`, `metrics::MODULE_PANEL`; changed: `TreeRow` is a label for a screen reader wherever it takes no click (it was where `dim`, which takes none), and `PanelHeader::show` lays its bar out in a child rather than a scope (a scope moved the cursor back up into the bar, and what followed the header went under it by 9 points; unused before T13). `mg-ui`: `outliner.rs` (new: `Outliner`, `area`, `take_keys`, `ui`; `OUTLINER`, public); `shell.rs`: `Panel::Outliner` (its dock, name, icon, strip tip, keys, `available`), `Panel::OUTLINER`, `editor_brings_outliner`, `MODULE_PANEL_ID`, `MODULE_PANEL_LEAST`, `OUTLINER_LEAST`, `left_dock` split (the upper panel always in a `Panel::top` of its own, resizable only over the Outliner, so that the tree's ids, open groups and scroll place stay as the Outliner comes and goes), `upper_panel`; `tree.rs`: one copy of the per-area code for both, `contents_ui` takes a `Look` (`Tree` or `Outliner`), `fresh_contents`, `act_on_object` (what a placed object's row asks, the tree's `go` moved out), `select_in_view`, `typed_row` (the type-ahead, moved out), `area_key_rows`, `step_in`, `seek_in`, `past_deleted_in` (the keys over any rows), `kind_count`, `eye_words`, `object_label`, `outliner_text`, `Asked::Select`; `area_view.rs`: `kinds_shown`, `show_kind` (the toolbar's `share_shown` through the same `share`); `palette_view::Keys::Outliner`; `Settings::module_panel_height` (and Reset Layout); `Moonglow::outliner_fold_all` (hidden, for `frame_perf`); a selection the Outliner makes itself (a click, Ctrl or Shift, Enter) is noted as seen and not scrolled to (`made_here`: a row cut by its foot stays under the pointer, a Ctrl+click does not scroll to the last chosen), only one made in the view or the tree is; its Escape is its own while it has the keys even with no row (an area with nothing placed); a placed object's Delete in a read-only area (the Outliner's key, and the tree's menu and key, one code) goes straight to the claims' gate, which refuses it and says why, the selection, the marks and the Properties tabs left as they were (they were cleared before the refusal); F6 goes Left Dock tab, Outliner, editor area, Right Dock, log. **Its contents** are the module tree's copy (`Moonglow::area_contents`), read again at once where the area's GIT changed (`read_of`: the open document's revision, else the stored bytes' stamp), which an edit, an Undo or Redo, a paste, a delete or a rename of an object (its name or tag) moves; counts are its rows' number, names and tags read once per change. **What a builder notices.** Under the Module and Resources tabs, beside an area, the Outliner: the area's objects by kind with counts and eyes; an eye and the toolbar's toggle are one; a click selects without moving the camera (Enter and the menu's Go To move it), a row of a hidden kind shows its kind; the seam is dragged and kept; the module tree is 496 points high at first beside an area, and its scroll place and open groups kept by egui from an earlier version start afresh once (its widgets' ids moved with the split). **Tests.** App `tests/app/outliner.rs` (new): `the_eye_and_the_area_toolbar_s_show_toggle_are_one_state`, `selecting_in_the_view_marks_and_reveals_the_row`, `a_click_on_a_row_selects_in_the_view`, `the_counts_follow_an_edit_and_undo`, `the_outliner_comes_and_goes_with_the_active_editor` (both modes), `the_seam_is_kept_across_a_restart_and_a_short_window`, `expand_all_and_collapse_all`, `the_outliner_s_names_for_a_screen_reader`, `the_outliner_s_keys`, `a_read_only_area_s_outliner_hides_and_deletes_nothing`, `a_click_on_a_row_leaves_the_outliner_still`; `open_editors.rs`'s `the_esc_hint_names_another_tab_than_the_active_editor[_as_tabs]` gives the Outliner the keys (no Esc hinted, its Escape leaves Module Properties open); kit: `an_outliner_row_s_eye_takes_its_own_click`, `a_row_s_weak_second_word_gives_way_first`, `what_follows_a_panel_header_goes_under_it`. Migrated: the F6 orders (`right_dock.rs`, `preview_panel.rs`), nine module tree tests given the tree's old height (`common::tall_tree`: the window 300 points taller, the seam at its lowest), `the_module_tree_stays_where_it_was_when_a_tab_closes` (the tree grows as the Outliner goes: its last row stays in sight, as low or lower); `settings_from_4_1…` names the new field; in `screens.rs`, `shell_resources_window` turns the wheel over the Resources list above the Outliner, `menus_tree_several_objects` gives the tree its old height, `grouped_context_menus` takes the Areas well to its top (Home) before the area row's menus (the waypoint selected had scrolled the well, five rows high over the Outliner). **Pictures** (`target/ui-mock/impl/m3-t13/`, before, dark, light): `shell_main`, `shell_wide`, `shell_narrow`, `shell_left_folded`, `shell_status_hints_*` (420 to 1600), and `shell_outliner` (new: the pyre selected, four kinds hidden; `shell_outliner_crop` and, after Collapse All, `shell_outliner_kinds_crop` beside `LightArea_crop_outl.png`: the same counts, quiet rows and struck eyes). **Timing.** `frame_perf` and `editor_perf` (release, `target/ui-mock/perf-m3/t13/`: the last run on the final tree is `frame_perf.log`, `editor_perf.log`, `compare.txt`; earlier runs `_run1` to `_run3`, loads in `loads.txt`) against T12's and M3b's: all pass; over the budget only the four known area084 views under the wheel (8.2 to 9.5 ms; T12's 8.4 to 10.0). `frame_perf` draws the Left Dock, so the Outliner is beside every area it times; added for it: area084 with the Outliner's Sounds (317 rows) opened, one selected, against its kinds closed with the same one selected: 0.76 and 0.77 ms (pointer, idle) against 0.76 and 0.70 (26,560 vertices against 25,468: the rows are drawn), no difference beyond the runs' spread. Reading area084's 549 objects again (after a GIT edit or Undo) takes 0.31 ms (a scratch timing, removed). No view is slower than T12's by more than 0.3 ms in the last run (the largest, area084's "all selected, walkmesh" with Sound Ranges on, 6.59 to 6.88 against 6.34 to 6.73; that view ran 6.0 to 7.2 across four runs, the first at a load of 3.7, and the Outliner keeps the selection's set from one change to the next rather than every frame since the third); many are faster by 0.3 to 1.3 (lake, area092 turning, area084 turning and close: the spread). `editor_perf`: within 0.3 ms of T12's but a table's Find with a cell set each frame (2.54 to 3.00 ms; T12 had moved it 3.12 to 2.54) and Select Texture's click (10.80 to 11.39), editors with no area in front and no Outliner, and a few faster (the check as you type 8.07 to 7.69, the huge script's open 22.71 to 22.01). **Inherits:** T14: the 420 window and the wide layout's seam (its Inherits list). T15: the Outliner's rows and the tree's share `contents_ui`; whether the module tree keeps its areas opened out (R2) is M4's ruling with the area editor. M4 (S5): each editor's Outliner (`Panel::available` by `active_editor`: a hak's contents, a 2DA's columns with an eye, a tileset's parts, the factions, a conversation's Bookmarks), the kit's `PanelHeader`, `TreeGroup::eye` and `TreeRow::second` for them; the area header's Show toggles (M4) beside the eyes, one state through `area_view::show_kind`; the Plugins menu's "pane last clicked" does not count the Outliner (the tree's does).

**Review, 2026-11-14.** An independent review of the commit found seven faults; each is fixed, with a test seen to fail with the fix taken out (the failure text in brackets). (1) **Delete in the Outliner could delete an object of another area** (data loss): the keys' row kept its area's place but the Delete took the area in front, and the row was never let go of when another area came in front. The Outliner now lets go of what it kept of the rows of the area it listed before (the keys' row, the anchor, a kind to open, what was typed: `Outliner::listed_area`), the keys going to the new area's first row where they are the Outliner's; Delete acts only on a row it lists (the one under the pointer, else the keys' row, which may be of a kind since closed); the anchor is per area. `outliner::nothing_acts_on_a_row_of_another_area`, both modes, two areas: A's row clicked, B in front, F6, Delete, Enter, a Shift+click in B ["never: B's kind opened by Enter", with the reset, the listed-row filter and the anchor's area taken out]. (2) **The Shift+click anchor went stale**: it is now `tree::Anchor`, one type for the module tree and the Outliner: an object in its area with how many of each kind the area held, holding only while none came or went (a delete, a paste, an Undo or Redo, an outside change), let go of as the selection changes elsewhere and on a delete. `the_shift_anchor_follows_the_selection_and_a_delete`: (b) a row deleted, Shift+click chooses from nothing ["never: CHEST_00 alone"], (a) another selected in the view, Shift+click goes from it ["never: from CHEST_04"]. The tree's own anchor goes the same way (`tree_objects_anchor` is an `Anchor`); its cases (a) and (b) already held (it takes the one selected when its set is cleared). (3) **The module tree at the window's size at first** (its Areas well 5 rows over the Outliner, Main.png's): `common::tall_tree` now waits for the seam to come down and rest, not a count of frames; `tree::the_module_tree_s_placed_objects_at_the_size_at_first` (1160 by 800, no `tall_tree`) scrolls the well with the wheel to the area's last kind and its rows, selects, Ctrl+clicks, deletes both by the menu (Undo), finds Export as Blueprint… on one's menu, and goes to an object by the keys (Right, Down, Enter): all of it works at that size. **The owner's ruling** on the question this raised, in the owner's words (2026-11-14): "Yes: while the Outliner shows, the tree stops following the selection." Built: `tree::follow_selection` marks the object (and moves the keys' row to it) but neither opens its area out nor scrolls to it while the Outliner is shown; the condition is the Outliner being drawn this frame, `Panel::OUTLINER.available` with the Left Dock unfolded (follow_selection runs only where the tree is drawn, before the Outliner, so availability and an unfolded dock are what "shown this frame" is at that point); without it (another tab in front, As Floating Windows with no area active) the tree follows as before. It acts on changes alone, so the tree does not catch up when the Outliner goes or comes back; a selection changed by Undo or a paste is a change made elsewhere; what the builder does in the tree (a click, its keys, Go To, type-ahead) and what asks the tree by name (Show in Module Tree, a tab chosen) still move it. Find Instance goes to the object in the area (its view and camera), not to the tree, so the Outliner shows it there. `outliner::with_the_outliner_shown_the_tree_stays_where_it_was` ["the tree's area still closed"], `tree::the_module_tree_sorts_objects_and_stays_still_under_a_click` (now both ways: the Outliner shown, the row stays out of view and marked ["not scrolled to"]; without it, scrolled to), `tree::the_module_tree_shows_the_object_selected_in_the_area` without the Outliner ["both waypoints' rows are there: 0 → 0" with the rule applied everywhere]. Migrated to what they mean now: `module_panel::an_object_selected_in_an_area_brings_its_row_into_the_well_s_view` and `tree::the_module_tree_shows_the_object_selected_in_the_area` (`common::outliner_away`: Module Properties docked in front), `area_view::shift_g_drops_the_selection_onto_the_placeable_below` and `outliner::the_outliner_s_names_for_a_screen_reader` (the area opened out by its arrow), `tree::the_module_tree_stays_where_it_was_when_a_tab_closes` (End over the well scrolls it). R2's text has a note. (4) **Object rows moved 14 points left with their kind's row scrolled out**: older than T13 (the kit's `TreeGroup` since T3; the tree's well showed it too): out of sight the group's row took no width, and egui's body, made as wide as that, was pulled left over its indent. The body is now made at least as wide as its indent reaches. Kit `a_group_s_rows_keep_their_indent_with_its_row_scrolled_out` ["the same indent: left 8, right 22"]; the indent is the board's (an object's name 36 points from the Outliner's left, `.tree-row.d1`). (5) **The tree's Delete key in a read-only area** moved the keys' row before the gate refused: it now stays. `tree::a_read_only_area_s_object_is_not_deleted_from_the_tree`, the key and the menu ["the keys' row as it was: left Kind, right Object"; "still selected: left []"]. (6) **One rule for a hidden kind**: going to or selecting an object of a kind hidden in the view, from the module tree (a click, Ctrl or Shift, Go To, Adjust Location) or the Outliner, shows that kind in every view, as its toggle would (`tree::show_kinds_of`, in `act_on_object` and the tree's Ctrl and Shift); before, the tree's click selected a hidden object, against `share`'s rule (older than T13). `tree::an_object_of_a_hidden_kind_gone_to_from_the_tree_shows_its_kind` ["never: shown and selected"; with Ctrl "never: shown and chosen"]. (7) **The least window**: the Outliner folds to its header (Expand All and Collapse All still on it) where the dock is too short for its 116 points and the 180 of the panel over it; its rows come back with the room (the Narrow board folds the whole dock, T14's yield). `outliner::the_outliner_folds_to_its_header_in_the_least_window` ["never: folded to its header"]. The tab-closes assertion is bounded by the Outliner's height. **Also checked**: Tab goes through the Outliner by named stops only, in order (Collapse All, each kind's eye, a kind with something placed its row before its eye), and Space and Enter on a focused eye hide and show its kind (`tab_goes_through_the_outliner_by_named_stops` ["a stop with no name" with the kinds unnamed]; the seam is a drag handle, no stop); the tree's open groups are kept across two restarts of the window given egui's memory (`the_tree_s_open_groups_are_kept_across_two_restarts` ["restart 1: still closed" with an id that changes from run to run]). The pictures (`target/ui-mock/impl/m3-t13/dark`, `light`) are made again: the tree no longer opens the area out beside the Outliner. **Timing** (`target/ui-mock/perf-m3/t13/review/`): `frame_perf` and `editor_perf` pass; over the budget the four known area084 views under the wheel (8.0 to 10.4 ms) and area084's "all selected, walkmesh" with Sound Ranges on (7.3 ms, within the test's margin). Both full runs (1-minute load 1.8 to 2.9, the 5-minute 2.7 to 4.0, the machine busier than for the Done paragraph's runs) put area084's views 0.3 to 0.9 ms slower than the commit's own run, so the commit itself was timed against them, interleaved, twice each (`review/ab/`, a `git archive` copy built apart): the commit's "all selected, walkmesh" with Sound Ranges on 6.41 to 6.92 ms against this tree's 6.43 to 7.26, turning 3.39 to 3.75 against 3.05 to 3.55, close 2.71 to 2.88 against 2.45 to 3.07: no difference beyond the runs' spread.

**T14. How the shell yields, and the wide layout.**

- **Goal.** Apply `Yields` (section 5.2):
  - below W 1300: Compile, Verify and Test Module lose their words (built by T2), ~~Quick Open is capped at 300 px and becomes an icon under 240~~ (built by T8, with the order it yields in against the build and quick actions);
  - below 1500: the Left Dock folds to its strip automatically, without writing `hide_tree`, and the user can unfold it;
  - below 1400: the Right Dock goes to 280 px with its panels as tabs, and the Status Bar switches become icons, then the game folder drops, then hints drop.

  Section 5.3 at W ≥ 2560 (U1): Left Dock 320 by default; the Right Dock stands two columns (Palette | Preview) when wider than 2 × 280.
- **Inherits** (left for T14 by T5 and its review, the owner's word of 2026-11-12):
  - ~~`the_area_toolbar_stays_inside_its_tab_at_every_width` folds the Left Dock by hand under 772 (760 until the review, when the Right Dock stayed at the 280 narrower windows had left it): it stops, the yields doing it;~~ (settled by T14: the sweep turns the yields on and folds nothing by hand);
  - ~~from 420 to 760, both docks set open, the editor area is left little (the least window about 24 points, 170 at 760): the yields give it its room;~~ (settled by T14: both docks strips under 796, the editor area 370 at 420 and 710 at 760; unfolded by hand, one dock at a time where both do not fit);
  - ~~from 420 to 500 the Bottom Dock's Log tab is over its Copy Log button~~ (settled by T7: the tab turns to its icon, the tools give way, a dock narrower than the icon draws its bar empty);
  - ~~the palette's type combo under 300 px of panel width (the strip wraps to rows meanwhile, down to the 190 the dock has in the least window);~~ (settled by T14: `palette_view::TYPE_COMBO_UNDER`);
  - ~~(from T7) in a 420 px window with both side docks set open the Bottom Dock is 24 points wide and the log's text wraps a character a line (its tab bar is drawn empty there, T7's review);~~ (settled by T14: as wide as the editor area, 370 at 420);
  - ~~(from T7) under 1400 the Status Bar's switches become icons (`StatusSwitch::icon_only`); the order the bar yields in is built in `shell::status_bar` (the last line, the game folder, the job's name, then the module's name), and `status_bar.rs`'s sweep holds it: the switches' icons go first in that order;~~ (settled by T14);
  - ~~(from T8) Quick Open's field yields within the Top Bar already (capped at 300 under 1300, an icon under 240, then the build actions, the icon and the quick actions in that order, `quick_open.rs`'s sweep); T14's tests at 1280, 1399, 1499 and 3440 name it by "Quick Open" in the Top Bar, a text field or an icon button; the Welcome board's Status Bar hint for Ctrl+P is T12's hints;~~ (settled by T14: its cap read from `Yields`; `what_the_shell_shows_at_each_width` names it);
  - ~~(from T13) the Outliner under the Left Dock's tabs: in the 420 by 600 window it holds its least (116 points: its header and four rows) and the Module panel the rest (430 of the 496 chosen; its own least is 180: the tab bar, the Filter, five rows), the seam held there and back where chosen in a taller window (`shell_status_hints_420.png`); the Left Dock folded by width keeps the strip's three icons (Module, Resources, the Outliner, as Narrow.png has them); the wide layout's seam: GalleryWide draws the Module panel 760 points high in a 1440 window where the Main board's 496 is kept today (`Settings::module_panel_height`, chosen or `metrics::MODULE_PANEL`);~~ (settled by T14);
  - ~~(from T6) the Right Dock's Palette and Preview are two tabs of one bar at every width: under 1400 nothing changes for them (they are tabs already); at W ≥ 2560 they stand two columns (Palette | Preview, U1) where the dock is wider than 560, each column then drawing its panel (the Preview's body in its column, as `PreviewSlot` hands it on).~~ (settled by T14, with the board's 741 at first there).
- **Not in M3.** The area header's compact form (E < 990), Snap and Turn text (E < 550) and More (E < 490) are M4's area header.
- **Tests.**
  - App at 1280, 1399, 1499 and 3440: what is shown, by name.
  - Pictures `shell_narrow` against Narrow.png and Narrow_crop_*.png; `shell_wide` against WideArea.png.
- **Risks.** `frame_perf` at 3440 (and 1280 with `FRAME_TARGET`).
- **Manual.** 02 (a paragraph on narrow and wide windows).

**Done, 2026-11-14.** **Plan against board** (the boards won where they differ): (1) Narrow.png's strip has a chevron at its top; the strip kept T5's button beside its edge instead (one control, where the fold button was: T5's ruled-out chevron, issue 10's rule), the three icons below as the board has them. *Changed by the review below: the chevron at the strip's top, the open dock's button at its tab bar's end.* (2) Neither the boards nor SPEC say what unfolding does in a narrow window: decided, it pushes the editor area as at any width (no overlay) and holds while the window is under the width that folds the dock; from there the builder's own fold has it, so a window narrowed again folds it again; `hide_tree` and `hide_palettes` are never written by it. (3) No board draws a window under 1280: decided by the editor area's least, the narrowest editor section 5.2 designs the header for (`EDITOR_LEAST`, 490): under 796 the Right Dock folds to its strip too (the Left Dock's strip, 490 and 280 beside it), and under 962 both side docks do not fit, so a dock unfolded by hand folds the other (the one unfolded last stays); the least window (420) is both strips and 370 of editor area. (4) WideArea's first column holds the Palette and the Preview as two tabs of one bar, and its second the Properties panel (M4's, S2); built as the plan says, Palette | Preview, so the Preview has moved out of its board column into the second, which stands empty of Properties until M4: when the Properties panel arrives, M4 puts the Preview back as a tab of the first column beside the Palette, and Properties in the second, as WideArea draws them. The first column is the board's 340 (half the dock where that is less), the dock 741 at first in the wide layout (the board's `--right-w`; U1 gave no default). (5) GalleryWide's 480 palette column in Gallery mode is the palette's own change (M4). (6) The palette's type combo: built as a dropdown with the type's icon under 300 points of panel; Narrow.png's Standard and Custom beside it, and Expand All and Collapse All at the Group By row's end, are M4's palette change. (7) The Status Bar's three switches on the board: M3 has the Log's alone (Problems and Search Results come with M4). (8) The flush editor area: the boards' 974 at 1280 depends on it, so built here (`CentralPanel` without egui's 8-point margin); the edge fold buttons, 12 points beside the docks, now stand over the editor area's first 12 points half way down (they stood over its margin before). **M4's parts of the boards**: Narrow.png's compact area header (Show 5 of 10, the moon, the icon popovers), the tool strip at the view's left, the Properties tab; WideArea's header unfolded into its row, the script's second group content, the Properties column. **Built.** Kit (`mg-uikit`, added): `shell::RIGHT_DOCK_WIDE`, `RIGHT_COLUMN`, `MODULE_PANEL_WIDE`, `EDITOR_LEAST`, `RIGHT_DOCK_STRIP_UNDER`, `Yields::right_dock_narrow`, `right_dock_folded`, `module_panel_height`, `Yields::right_first_column`; changed: `Yields::right_dock_width` is 741 in the wide layout. `mg-ui`: `shell::frame_yields` (once a frame, `Moonglow::yields`), `left_folded`, `right_folded`, `folded_by_width`, `set_dock_open` (every read and write of a side dock's fold), `dock_tip` (View's rows and the edge buttons in a narrow window), `take_dock_sizes`, `chosen_size(kept, default)` (nothing stored until a drag), `right_body`, `right_reserved`, `BOTH_DOCKS_UNDER`; public `RIGHT_COLUMN_TWO` (the second column's tab bar), hidden `Moonglow::docks_fold_by_width`; `Settings::dock_sizes_taken`; `palette_view::TYPE_COMBO_UNDER`; the Top Bar and Quick Open read `Moonglow::yields`; `step_pane` stops at a dock folded by the width and unfolds it, and at each of the two columns (`Moonglow::right_columns`), whose panel, as one pressed in, becomes the dock's; `tree::follow_selection` reads `left_folded`; View › Reset Layout stores no sizes. **The thresholds**: W < 1300 the build actions' words go, Quick Open at most 300 (T2, T8); W < 1400 the Right Dock 280 (edge not dragged), its panels tabs, the switches icons, the game folder gone; W < 1500 the Left Dock a strip; W < 962 one side dock open at a time; W < 796 the Right Dock a strip; panel < 300 the palette's types a dropdown; W ≥ 2560 the Left Dock 320, the Right Dock 741, two columns where wider than 560, the seam 760, each where not chosen. **The Status Bar's yield order** (as in `status_bar`'s comment and the manual): the switches' icons; the game folder; the hints whole from the end; the last line down to 48 points, then gone; the counts from the end; the job's name; the module's name. **The Outliner rule** (the owner's ruling in T13's review): "the Outliner shown this frame" is the Left Dock as drawn (`left_folded`), not `hide_tree`: folded by the width, neither the tree nor the Outliner is drawn and the tree moves for nothing (no catching up when it comes back); unfolded there by hand, `hide_tree` on or off, the Outliner shows and the tree stays still. **What a builder notices.** At 1280 the Left Dock is its strip, the Right Dock 280 with the types in a dropdown, the switches icons, no folder, the editor area flush with the bars (974); at 1920 the Main layout, the editor area 16 points wider and 16 taller than before (flush); at 3440 the Left Dock 320, the Right Dock 741 with the Palette and the Preview side by side, the Module panel 760 high. The sizes this branch stored before anything was dragged (260, 320, 180) and 4.2.0's untouched 240 and 120 are taken as not chosen once. **Tests.** Kit: `the_shell_yields_at_the_measured_widths` (the new fields and constants). App, `tests/app/yields.rs` (new): `what_the_shell_shows_at_each_width` (both modes, 1280, 1399, 1499, 1600, 3440, by name), `every_width_is_whole_and_the_editor_keeps_its_least` (420 to 3440 by 20, both modes), `a_window_narrowed_and_widened_writes_nothing_and_gives_all_back`, `a_dock_folded_by_the_width_unfolds_until_the_window_is_wide_again`, `both_docks_where_they_do_not_fit_and_f6`, `sizes_kept_by_older_versions_are_taken_once`, `the_palette_s_types_are_a_dropdown_in_the_narrow_dock`, `a_resize_leaves_the_keys_where_they_are`, `the_wide_dock_s_columns_by_the_keys_and_a_chosen_blueprint` (the chest's model in the Preview's column at once, F6's stop at each column, a press in a column), `a_dock_unfolded_by_hand_in_a_narrow_window_is_whole` (420 to 1500 by 40, the Left Dock then the Right Dock unfolded by hand); `outliner::a_dock_unfolded_by_hand_in_a_narrow_window_keeps_the_tree_still`. Each seen failing with its guard broken: (`Yields::right_dock_columns` false) "Floating 3440: left (…, \"tabs\", …) right (…, \"columns\", …)", and with the switches' icons by room alone "Floating 1280: … false … true" (`what_the_shell_shows_at_each_width`); the Right Dock never folded by the width, "Floating 420: the editor area 99 under 370"; the narrow window storing the Right Dock's drawn width, "written at 1340"; `left_unfolded_here` never let go of, "folded again by the narrow window"; an unfold by hand writing `hide_tree`, "the builder's fold kept"; no dock folded where both do not fit, "left: (\"open\", \"tabs\") right: (\"open\", \"strip\")"; F6's unfold taken out, "F6 unfolds the Left Dock folded by the width"; the sizes taken every frame, "nothing taken from what was drawn: left: Some(410) right: None"; `TYPE_COMBO_UNDER` 0, "no row at 280: left: 13 right: 0"; the keys given to the area as the dock folds, "kept with the dock folded by the width: left: \"Area\" right: \"Tree\""; `follow_selection` reading `hide_tree`, "the tree's area still closed" (the Outliner's test); F6 with one stop for the two columns, "the Preview's column next: … left: \"Log\" right: \"Preview\""; a press in a column not taken, "left: Some(Preview) right: Some(Palette)"; no dock folded where both do not fit, by hand, "420 Right: the Right Dock 190 wide", "420 Right: both docks open where they do not fit". (The Status Bar sweep's new "under 1400" check alone does not fail with the icons' rule broken: its long texts make the room rule give the icons there too; the shown test does.). Migrated: the old tests run with `Moonglow::docks_fold_by_width` off (`common::docks_as_set`, in each harness builder and the ten tests that build their own), as though both docks were unfolded by hand; `windows::each_pane_folds_by_one_button_on_its_inner_edge` at 1400 (the narrow dock is not dragged) and its margin 8 to 0 (flush: the same equalities); `right_dock::the_right_dock_keeps_its_width_through_a_narrow_window` asserts the narrow 280 at 900 and 700 exactly, the edge not dragged there and the width kept (it asserted "narrower"; no window from 1400 holds 470 narrower, so the drag that was at 900 is at 1400); the two "width kept by an earlier run" tests mark the settings as an older version's (`dock_sizes_taken` false), as that is what they mean; three Right Dock tests at 1400 (the palette's toolbar under 280 wraps below where they read the list); `the_least_window_holds_both_docks` opens the palette's groups (its types are one dropdown there now), its "more than 100 nodes" kept; five places read the palette's types as the row or the dropdown (`common::palette_types`); `status_bar::with_no_line_yet_nothing_is_shown_or_kept` sweeps 1400 to 2200 with a longer folder and module (under 1400 the folder goes whatever the room); a Reset Layout stores no height (`None`, not 180); the Status Bar sweep gains the switches' order and stage (stronger); the area toolbar's sweep turns the yields on and folds nothing by hand; `tiles::tile_variant_pictures_take_the_size_set` accepts the picture and the gap with or without the button's padding (a grid's column is as wide as its framed picture, which a wider window moved out of the first two columns: one exact value before, a range of 8 points now, still apart from the other size); `transition_setup`'s Target Area box is the window's, not the area toolbar's behind it on the same row, and its row is under the box; `common::click_tab_close` takes the close button at the tab's right end (another tab's under a floating window lay within its rectangle once the editor area's tab bar was flush) and `fields::closing_a_window_cancels_its_changes_when_asked` waits for the close with a deadline; the settings' new-installation test names `dock_sizes_taken`.. **Pictures** (`target/ui-mock/impl/m3-t14/`, before, dark, light): `shell_narrow`, `shell_wide`, `shell_main`, `shell_left_folded`, `shell_760`, `shell_status_hints_*`, and `shell_width_<w>` (new: 420 to 3440). **Timing.** `frame_perf` and `editor_perf` (release, `target/ui-mock/perf-m3/t14/`: `frame_perf.log` is the third run, `_run1` and `_run2` the others, each with its `compare` against T13's and M3b's, the loads in `loads.txt`): all pass at 3440 by 1400; over the budget the known area084 overview and turning under the wheel (8.2 to 10.6 ms), and in runs 1 and 3 (the GPU shared) area084's "all selected, walkmesh" (7.0 to 7.2 ms, within the test's margin) and in run 1 its one-column view under the wheel (7.5). The GPU was shared with another session in runs 1 and 3 (the area views' GPU times 10 to 50 ms where T13's were 1 to 5), and the CPU times of those views rose with it. In run 2 (load 1.4 to 1.7) the views more than 0.3 ms off T13's: slower, area084's overview and turning under the wheel (+0.7, +1.4; +0.5 in run 1; M3b's 8.8 and 10.0), its tiles palette searched as typed under the wheel (+1.2, 5.22 against M3b's 5.76), its "all selected, walkmesh" (+0.5 to +0.7, M3b 6.27), "area, a creature, the Palette tab in front" (+0.3: the wide layout's two columns draw the Preview's creature beside the Palette), "field, high, no grass" under the wheel (+0.4); faster, the palettes searched or with 8,000 shown under the wheel (-0.6, -0.7), where in runs 1 and 2 the wheel went to the Preview's column (`frame_perf`'s palette rectangle now takes the first column: run 3, 2.12 against 2.53). Timed against T13's tree interleaved (`perf-m3/t14/ab/`, a `git archive` copy built apart, `sound_ranges_frames_at_144` twice each, loads 2 to 6), area084's views that were slower in all three runs: overview under the wheel 8.61 and 8.33 ms against 8.19 and 8.09, turning under the wheel 9.68 and 9.32 against 9.96 and 9.76, "all selected, walkmesh" 6.73 (and an outlier of 12.65) against 6.13 and 7.04: the runs' spread; the tiles palette searched as typed under the wheel 5.42 and 5.80 against 5.16 and 4.89, 0.3 to 0.9 ms slower, the palette's column now 340 wide with the Preview's column drawn beside it. The two columns against one at 3440 (area084, a blueprint chosen, new): 2.48 against 2.33 ms with the pointer moving, 2.47 against 2.28 idle, 4.85 against 5.24 under the wheel (run 2): the Preview's model beside the area costs about 0.15 ms. At 1280 by 800 (`FRAME_SCREEN=1280x800`, new; the Left Dock unfolded by hand for the views that click it): area084's overview and turning under the wheel 8.1 and 9.4 ms, as at 3440, its other views under 3 ms; one view over: the custom item palette with all 8,000 rows under the wheel, 8.8 to 9.4 ms in the 280 dock, against 2.6 at 1400 (a 320 dock) and 2.7 in T13's tree at 1280 (its dock 320 there). *Corrected by the review below: not the dock's width but the hovered row's picture asked for each frame, the pointer on the rows' words in a 280 dock and past their end in a 320 one; fixed there.* `editor_perf`: within 0.3 ms of T13's but the huge script's open (+1.3), first frame (+5.8 of 261), everything typed over (+0.4) and taken back (+1.7 of 110), and faster a table's Find with a cell set each frame (-0.6) and Select Texture's click (-0.7): editors with no shell change of their own, the runs' spread. **Inherits:** T15: (the palette under the wheel, the edge fold buttons and the old tests' hook were settled by the review below) the old tests' `docks_fold_by_width` off (about 160 tests look at dock contents in windows under 1500: migrating them to the yields one by one, or keeping the hook, is the closing pass's call); the Right Dock's columns' seam is a line, not dragged. M4: the area header's compact form, the tool strip and the Properties tab (Narrow.png), the Properties column (WideArea), the palette's dropdown row with Standard and Custom beside it and Expand All at the Group By row's end, GalleryWide's 480 Gallery column, Problems and Search Results switches as icons.

**Review, 2026-11-14.** An independent review of T14 (its commit `d6b04e1f`) found ten faults; each is fixed with a test seen to fail with the fix taken out (the failure text in brackets). (1) **Show in Module Tree did nothing visible at the app's first size** (1280 by 800, the Left Dock folded by the width): it set the reveal and the panel but never unfolded the dock. It goes through `show_dock_panel` now (`set_dock_open`), as Tools › Resource Browser, Palettes and Preview do. Audited, every command that asks a dock's panel on purpose: Show in Module Tree, Tools › Resource Browser, Tools › Palettes, Tools › Preview, the strips' buttons and chevrons, View › Left Dock and Right Dock and their keys unfold the dock; the tree and the Resources panel reveal rows only within a drawn dock (the tree's following and the browser's keys); no command opens the log on an error; Quick Open and Find Instance open tabs or go to the area, asking no dock; the plugins open no dock panel; a blueprint chosen in the palette shows in the Preview only where its dock is drawn; the Outliner's reveal is within its drawn dock. `module_panel::show_in_module_tree_from_a_tab_s_menu_reaches_into_the_well_and_below` now folds the dock in its 1000 window before each Show in Module Tree ["the dock unfolded"]; `yields::a_panel_asked_for_by_name_unfolds_its_dock` (1280 and 700). (2) **The tests' hook hid the default user's layout**: `docks_fold_by_width` is gone, the yields on in every harness. No test turns the yields off now: UI tests unfold the Left Dock as a builder would at 109 places in 25 files (its strip's chevron, `common::unfold_left_dock`, some of them in shared set-ups that more tests go through), those that read or write the builder's own fold run in a 1600 window at 20 places (`common::wide_window`), the Right Dock is unfolded at 7 (`unfold_right_dock`), and `screens.rs` unfolds by hand at 11 places, the 9 cases the hook had served among them; the app's 1338 tests (34 ignored) pass with the yields on. Faults the migration found in the product: (1) above, (5) and (10) below; and in the tests, `common::click_tab_close` could take another tab's close square once a dock folded (it takes the rightmost one in the tab now). (3) **The keys and a dock not drawn** (the orchestrator's decisions, reversible on the owner's word): (a) when a dock holding the arrow keys is folded, by the width or by hand, they go to the editor area's active editor (`shell::keys_leave_folded_docks`, `Moonglow::editor_keys`) and are not handed back when it unfolds; (b) F6 moves only among what is drawn: it no longer unfolds a dock folded by the width (the one-way unfolding and, under 962, the layout flipping at each round, are gone); a folded dock's strip buttons are reached by Tab and named, and View's rows and their keys unfold it. `yields::keys_leave_a_dock_that_folds` ["the editor's with the dock folded by the width: left: \"Tree\" right: \"Area\""]; `yields::both_docks_where_they_do_not_fit_and_f6` [with the unfolding put back: "F6 never unfolds it: [\"Palette\", \"Log\", \"Tree\"]"]. (4) **The 1280 palette cost was not the dock's width**: the custom item palette asked for the hovered row's picture every frame, and under a resting pointer the wheel puts another row under it each frame, so a picture was made each frame; `frame_perf` turned the wheel at the dock's middle, on the rows' words in a 280 dock and past their end in a 320 one. Older than T14. The picture (and what is said of the blueprint) is asked for once the pointer has rested on one row for the tooltip's delay (`PaletteView::hover_since`); the tree, the Resources panel, the Outliner and the galleries ask for no picture of a hovered row. `right_dock::a_wheel_over_the_palette_asks_for_no_pictures_of_the_rows_it_passes` ["pictures asked for as the list scrolled: left: 37 right: 0"]; `frame_perf`'s palette rectangle turns the wheel 60 points in from the panel's left at every width. At 1280 by 800 `frame_perf` passes now (8 of 8, `FRAME_SCREEN=1280x800`; it failed at T14's commit): the custom item palette's 8,000 rows under the wheel 1.76 ms against 9.39 at the commit; at the default size 1.83 against T13's 2.53 (`perf-m3/t14/review/`, 1-minute load 2.6 to 3.3 after a 20-minute wait for it to fall under 3). (5) **Restoring open tabs wrote the Right Dock's fold**: a record names which panel each dock had in front, never how the builder left the dock, so restoring puts the Palette (or Resources) in front and writes no fold. `windows::a_tab_kept_in_the_palettes_pane_opens_where_its_kind_opens` ["the builder's fold stands"]. (6) **The wide layout's difference, fully** (the Done paragraph's item 4 now says it): WideArea.png's first column holds Palette and Preview as tabs and its second Properties; the build put the Preview in the second column, out of its board column; when Properties arrives (M4), the Preview goes back to a tab of the first column. (7) **One stored width served both layouts**: the Right Dock's width dragged in the wide layout is `Settings::right_dock_width_wide`, apart from `right_dock_width` below 2560 (old files map to the narrow one), each used in its own layout. The Left Dock's width and the Module panel's seam keep one value each: no threshold of theirs depends on the width (checked: a Left Dock of 300 is 300 in either layout, a seam of 400 holds in either). `yields::each_layout_keeps_its_own_right_dock_width` (dragged to 400 at 1600 and 700 at 3440, crossing 2560 twice each way) ["the wide layout's own: left: 399.0 right: 741.0"]. (8) **Sizes taken once could lose a real choice**: only a whole set counts as sizes at first, the tree's among them: this branch's 260, 320, 180 (or with 4.2.0's 240 and 120), and 4.2.0's untouched 240 and 120; a lone 320 with nothing else is kept, and a file with one of a set dragged keeps all. What can still be lost: a builder's drag to exactly every size of a set. *Changed by the second review below: each dock's sizes at first in any mix, or one alone (a lone 320 too), count.* `yields::sizes_kept_by_older_versions_are_taken_once` ["one of it dragged: all kept: left: (None, None, None) right: (Some(260), Some(400), Some(180))"]. (9) **Tests tightened**: `tiles::tile_variant_pictures_take_the_size_set` asserts the exact gap for the column the window gives (the chosen picture's column worked out from where it is drawn) [with the pictures 3 points wider: "84 points and the gaps: 93"]; the Status Bar sweep has a short-text pass from 1300 to 1500 where only the window's rule turns the switches to icons ["1300, short texts: icons false, folder None"]; `a_dock_unfolded_by_hand_in_a_narrow_window_is_whole` holds the editor area to its least where the window allows it and to what the dock at its least and the other's strip leave it where it does not [with the narrow dock 60 points wider: "420 Right: the editor area 39 under 115"]. (10) **The edge fold buttons took the editor area's clicks**: the boards draw no fold control for an open dock and a chevron at the folded strip's top (`Narrow.png`, `Narrow.html`'s rail); built so: the open dock's fold button at its tab bar's end, inside the dock (the Left Dock at its bar's end toward the editor area, after the Module panel's buttons; the Right Dock at its bar's end by the window's edge, after its tabs; in two columns this round put it on the first column's bar, mid-dock, which the second review below moved to the last column's bar's outer end), and folded, the chevron at the strip's top, its panels' buttons under it; named and tipped as before (`dock_tip`). No scroll bar of a tab's body is under a control now. `windows::each_pane_folds_by_one_button_on_its_inner_edge` [the Left Dock's button taken out: "one button for the the Left Dock: []"]. One consequence: with the fold button on its bar, the Left Dock's tabs show their icons up to about 262 points, at its first 260 too (Main.png draws their words): a board difference, the price of a fold control that takes nothing of the editor area. *A conflict to put to the owner*: T4's fix round (issue 10's "in the same place, folded") had put the strip's control beside its edge and taken the strip's chevron away; the orchestrator's reading of the boards brings the chevron back and the open dock's control to its tab bar, so the two are no longer in the same place. Tips reworded per the style guide: "Show the Left Dock in this narrow window", "Fold the Left Dock back to its strip". Pictures, Dark and Light, from 420 to 3440 and at 1280 and 420 with a dock unfolded by hand (`target/ui-mock/impl/m3-t14-review/`), looked at (Dark and Light at 796, 1600 and 2559, Dark at 962, the dock unfolded by hand at 1280 and 420 in both): each open dock's fold button at its tab bar's end, the strip's chevron at its top, nothing over the editor area. The default-size run found one fault of (7) in `frame_perf` itself: its one-column view set the narrow layout's width in the wide window and waited for the columns to end (it sets `right_dock_width_wide` now). **Timing against T13's tree** (a `git archive` copy built apart, interleaved, twice each, load 2.6 to 3.1): the tiles palette searched as typed under the wheel in the wide layout (area084, 3440) 4.94 and 5.09 ms against 5.00 and 4.36 with Sound Ranges off, 5.27 and 5.24 against 4.56 and 5.18 with them on: +0.34 and +0.39 ms on the means, within the runs' spread (other views of the same runs move by up to 0.43 either way); over all 237 views of `frames_at_144`, `game_shaders_frames_at_144` and `sound_ranges_frames_at_144`, the mean difference is 0.00 ms. Of the Done paragraph's "0.3 to 0.9 ms slower", the low end stands (+0.3 to +0.4 on that view, in both Sound Ranges states, two runs each, T13's own pair 0.64 apart) and the 0.9 does not. The full runs put the areas' views 0.3 to 0.5 ms over T13's full run (machine load; the interleaved runs do not). `editor_perf` (3 of 3; its sizes are its own, 1280 by 800 among them, with no size switch): its medians within about 0.2 ms of T13's run, the one-shot figures a little apart (opening the huge script 23.5 against 22.0 ms, everything typed over 6.6 against 6.2, the accessibility tree's typing 24.9 against 24.5, the texture list's click 10.9 against 11.4).

**Second review, 2026-11-14.** An independent review of the review's commit (`8b9a14d2`) found nineteen faults; each is fixed, the tests' and the product's seen to fail with the behavior broken in the source (the failure text in brackets). **The key fact under (1) to (9)**: below 1500 `shell::folds_by_choice` reads `left_unfolded_here` and never `hide_tree` (the Right Dock likewise below 796), so a test that writes `hide_tree` or `hide_palettes` in a narrower window changes nothing; tests of the builder's fold run in `common::wide_window`, tests of a narrow window fold by the dock's fold button and unfold by the strip's chevron. (1) `resources_panel::tools_resources_and_its_key_show_the_panel_folded_or_not`: its key unfolds a dock folded by the width (1280, folded by its button) and, in the 1600 window, one folded by the builder ["left: None, right: Some(Resources)" with `set_dock_open` writing no `hide_tree`]. (2) `the_left_dock_s_tabs_switch_by_click_keyboard_and_strip` folds the dock by its button before each strip button and asserts it folded ["Resources from the strip: left: None, right: Some(Resources)" with `show_dock_panel` not unfolding]. (3) `dialogs::a_dialog_opens_in_the_middle_of_the_main_window_whatever_the_docks` runs at 1600 and asserts each case's docks as set ["folded false false: [[471 140] - [809 661]] about [800 400]" with the dialog centered beside the Right Dock]. (4) `tree::resources_deleted_or_renamed_are_chosen_no_more` at 1600, the tree shown then folded, asserted ["tree folded away: false; left: [one, three, two], right: [three]" with the pruning only where the tree is folded]. (5) `right_dock::the_least_window_holds_both_docks` is `the_least_window_holds_the_right_dock_beside_the_left_dock_s_strip` (what it holds: under 962 one dock at a time); the always-true assertion is gone for one that the Right Dock alone shows, and the lost checks are back where they apply, the Left Dock's chevron and the Right Dock's fold button apart, the Left Dock's first ["at [420 320]: the Right Dock alone (Some(Module), Some(Palette))" with no dock folded where both do not fit]. (6) `windows::each_pane_folds_by_one_button_on_its_inner_edge` is `each_side_dock_folds_by_one_button_on_its_tab_bar`: it clicks the buttons with the pointer where they are drawn, so a covered button fails it, and asserts each at its bar's right end; "the the" mended ["the Left Dock: folded by its button" with a widget over the fold button]. (7) `icon_tips::every_icon_only_button_has_a_name_and_a_tooltip` walks the dock at 190 with a list of its own, so that the tabs are seen there as icons, not passed over as seen at 260 ["the icon tabs walked at 190: [...]" with the tabs' icons only over 200 points, which the commit's check let through]. (8) `status_bar::the_bottom_dock_s_tab_and_tools_never_overlap` sweeps from 300: since the yields the Bottom Dock is about 100 points at its narrowest in the 420 window, where the tab's icon keeps two tools, so the stages ("icon", 1), ("icon", 0) and ("none", 0) are reached only in a window under the least the app asks for (a window manager need not hold it; the harness does not); all are checked again ["(\"none\", 0) never seen" with the tab kept where it does not fit]. (9) `doc_tabs::the_tab_bar_holds_at_every_width` unfolds the Left Dock from 962 and asserts it shown there and not under it, the editor group's bar beside it ["never: the Left Dock unfolded" (its unfolding at 1020) with `folds_by_choice` not reading an unfolding by hand]; the widths under 280 of `right_dock.rs` and `preview_panel.rs` stay gone with the 280 least. (10) **The helpers**: `common::unfold_left_dock` and `unfold_right_dock` (one `unfold_dock`) fail where the dock is neither open nor folded with its chevron and unless it shows a panel after, and say that under 962 the other dock folds; `wide_window` settles; `click_tab_close`'s tabs and squares are those of the bar's own layer (the main window's for a docked tab; for a floating one its window's, the window whose area holds the bar, of overlapping windows the one with as many tabs there as its pane, else the first by its id, not by the order egui keeps its layers in), so a floating window's tabs over the bar no longer shift it (`shell.rs`'s `tab_title`, which walked every layer the same way, now takes `common::tab_rects`) ["the Left Dock neither open nor with its chevron" with the strip drawn without its chevron, where the old helper returned and the test went on; `doc_tabs::a_docked_tab_closes_by_its_button_with_a_window_s_tab_over_its_bar`, the Faction Editor's window over the editor area's bar left of the manual's tab, "its close button" with every layer's tabs counted]. One test the stricter helper caught: `modules::new_module_and_area_through_the_wizards` unfolded a dock with no module open. (11) `Moonglow::editor_keys` and `step_pane` each have their own doc. (12) `screens.rs`: `frames_until`'s doc whole, `unfold_left_dock`'s apart, and the WideArea tabs' doc, stray since before T14, on `board_tabs`. (13) **The palette's hover picture** is asked for after `palette_view::HOVER_REST` (0.12 s, well under egui's half-second tooltip delay, so the tooltip opens with its picture made or on its way), not the tooltip's whole delay; the wheel still asks for none (`a_wheel_over_the_palette_asks_for_no_pictures_of_the_rows_it_passes` passes); `right_dock::a_rested_row_s_picture_is_asked_for_before_its_tooltip_opens` ["asked for 0.500 s after the rest, the tooltip's delay 0.5" with the rest set to the delay]. (14) **The keys and a panel not drawn**: `shell::keys_leave_folded_docks` is `keys_leave_panels_not_drawn` and meets its doc for every panel that stops being drawn: a folded dock's, the Right Dock's beside a tab that brings it no panel (the Palette's keys stayed), the Outliner's when the area's tab goes (they stayed); a dock drawn on its other tab hands its keys to that tab's panel, the Left Dock's as it did (`left_dock`; `f6_steps_into_and_out_of_the_resources_panel` holds it) and now the Right Dock's too (the Palette's to the Preview and back; they had stayed with the Palette behind the Preview). `yields::keys_leave_a_panel_no_longer_drawn` ["Palette: the editor's, the Right Dock holds no panel: left \"Palette\", right \"Area\""; "Outliner: the editor's, no Outliner: left \"Outliner\", right \"Area\""; "with the Right Dock's tab: left \"Area\", right \"Preview\"" with the hand-over taken out]. (15) **Restoring open tabs and the Left Dock**: the code already wrote no fold (`left_panel` alone); the guard is `windows::an_old_record_with_the_resource_browser_shows_the_panel`, now also at 1400 (still its strip) and at 1600 with the builder's fold (it stands; View › Left Dock unfolds it on Resources) ["still folded by the width: left Some(Resources), right None" with `show_dock_panel` back]. (16) **The sizes at first, per dock**: `SIZES_AT_FIRST` is each dock's sizes at first: the tree's 260 (this branch from T5's review, `4fe9e132`, to T13: stored on the first frame the dock was drawn) or 240 (every release to 4.2.0, its egui panel's default, which this branch then stored), the Right Dock's 320 (this branch alone), the log's 180 or 120; read from `git log -p` of the settings and defaults on this branch and develop (every release tag's tree and log panels' defaults are 240 and 120; the kit's `LEFT_DOCK`, `RIGHT_DOCK` and `BOTTOM_DOCK` are 260, 320 and 180 unchanged since the kit's first commit, and every `chosen_size` that stored them, `4fe9e132` to T13, took them as its default). Each was stored apart where its dock was drawn, so an untouched file holds any mix, (240, 320, 180) and (260, 320, 120) among them, or one alone (the tree folded, no area open, a new installation's log closed since T7). Where every size kept is one of its dock's, none is taken as chosen; any other size keeps them all. A lone value is now dropped whichever dock's it is, 320 too (it was kept, and a lone 260 or 240 dropped): a lone 320 is what this branch wrote with only the Right Dock drawn. What can still be lost: a drag of every dock kept to exactly a size at first. `yields::sizes_kept_by_older_versions_are_taken_once` gains both mixed sets (and each with the tree dragged, kept), a 240 stored with 4.2.0's 120 kept by egui, each size alone, a lone 330 kept, and the wide layout's Left Dock 320 after an untouched mixed set ["a mixed set at first: (Some(240), Some(320), Some(180)): left (Some(240), Some(320), Some(180)), right (None, None, None)" with the whole-set rule back]. (17) **The fold button in two columns** is at the outer end of the last column's tab bar, by the window's edge, as in one column (it stood mid-dock at the first column's bar's end, where WideArea draws none); the one-column Right Dock's and the Left Dock's are where they were. `yields::the_wide_dock_s_columns_by_the_keys_and_a_chosen_blueprint` asserts it on the second column's bar after its tab, at the dock's right ["on the last column's bar, after its tab: [[3008 41] - [3030 63]] [[3033 39] - [3100.2 65]]" with it back on the first]. (18) **Docs**: the Review's fix (10) and U3 say what is built (the Left Dock's button at its bar's end toward the editor area, the Right Dock's at its bar's end by the window's edge, in two columns the last column's bar); stale names and docs mended: `resources_panel::a_press_on_a_list_s_scroll_bar_scrolls_it_and_folds_nothing` (was `…_by_the_fold_button_scrolls_it`), `windows::tab_reaches_the_right_dock_s_fold_button_after_its_controls`'s doc (no "round the window"), `shell.rs`'s module doc and `right_dock`'s, the drawing order's comment, `right_dock::drag_edge` and `a_press_on_the_palette_s_scroll_bar_scrolls_it`, `windows::panels_fold_away_and_come_back`, `common::right_dock_rect`; the manual (02) says where the Right Dock's arrow is in one and two columns and which panels the keys leave. (19) **Dead kit code removed**: `edge_fold_button`, `edge_unfold_button`, `edge_fold_rect`, `edge_fold_rect_beside`, `EDGE_FOLD_WIDTH` and with them `EDGE_FOLD_LENGTH` and the private `edge_button`, and their two kit tests; nothing in this worktree or in Moonglow Viewer (`grep -rn` over both) used them. `FoldStrip::chevron` stays (its kit test uses it), its doc no longer naming the edge button. **Pictures** (`target/ui-mock/impl/m3-t14-fix2/`, Dark and Light, `shell_widths` from 420 to 3440 and `shell_wide`), looked at against `8b9a14d2`'s: at 2560 and 3440 and in `shell_wide`, in both themes, the Right Dock's fold button is at the right end of the Preview's column's bar, by the window's edge, and nothing stands at the end of the Palette's column's bar; at 1600 nothing moved. Not timed again: the palette's hover picture (13) asks for one picture per rested row, as before, sooner.

**The owner's ruling, 2026-11-14:** "Fold controls and keys, keep." The docks' fold controls stay as built (an open dock's arrow on its tab bar, a folded strip's chevron at its top), superseding issue 10's "in the same place, folded"; and the keys leave a panel no longer drawn for the dock's other drawn tab, else the editor area, as (14) of the second review built.

**T15. Closing pass.**

- **Goal.**
