  - **The icon mesh cache, decided by measure.** If the shell's median view cost rises more than about 0.1 ms, or tessellation grows more than 25% over the baseline, build the cache keyed by icon, size, color and the font atlas's size (proposal line 782). Otherwise record the numbers and the decision in the proposal.
  - Replace README's `docs/images/area.jpg` from `readme_screenshots` (U2).
  - (from T9's review, the owner's word) a crowded bar can show several tabs that read alike once cut ("at_m1q4…" several times): accepted for now; a question for the closing pass: cut in the middle, so that a name's end shows?
  - (from T9) the document tabs' icons are tessellated each frame (32 tabs about 20 µs a frame more than before T9): the icon cache's measure includes them; `vendor/egui_dock` is dropped once egui_dock offers its two additions.
  - Tick the Where Things Moved rows done in M3.
  - Close the deferred entries the shell supersedes (issue 10's palettes and arrow parts, deferred.md:921-944; issues 11, 20 and 23, :957-975; the window bar, :1701), each with its reason.
  - Sweep the manual and `docs/parity/checklists.md` (§1 "Main menus…", :315-345 naming `TOOLBAR`; "Area viewer" :261).
- **Merge point M3c.**

**Done, 2026-11-14.** **Frame times** (`target/ui-mock/perf-m3/t15/`): the machine was shared, so the T0 tree (`d7f03d88`, built apart from a `git archive` copy) and T15's were run interleaved, T0, T15, T15, T0 (`ab2/`; the load sampled at each run's start, 1.4 to 2.5, though `ab2/loads.txt` ends at 9.61, so the last run's load rose during it; the first interleaved set, `ab1/`, started at 2.3 to 10.4, gave tessellation +4.4%: under the plan's 25% either way, and the decision stands), and a view counts as moved only where both pairs agree beyond 0.3 ms. Of 306 views in both, the median is 1.62 ms against 1.55 (−0.06), tessellation +2.3% summed; moved in both pairs: faster, the resource browser of 300,000 (1.7 to 2.0 ms, now 0.48: T4's list), the placeable palette searched for "a" (wheel, 1.0 to 0.56), area092 all selected with the walkmesh (idle and wheel, 0.3 to 1.2 less), area092's overview without the game's shaders (wheel, −0.4) and the grass field orbiting (−0.4 to −1.5); slower, area084's tiles palette searched as typed with Sound Ranges off (wheel, 3.93 and 5.03 against 5.28 and 5.42), as T14's review measured it against T13. A run alone at a load of 2.3 to 3.8 against the T0 quiet log of 2026-11-11, `m3b/` and `t14/review/` moves the same views, but its tessellation is 19.9% over that log's (`m3b` was 16.7%, the T14 review 17.8%) where the interleaved runs give 2.3%: the old log was taken on another day under another load, and only the interleaved pairs, run minutes apart on the same machine, compare like with like (the vertex counts, which load does not touch, agree: the interface views' median 13,510 against 13,494 in the first, noisy pair). Its two outliers (Area Properties 3.3 ms, area084's one-column Palette idle 8.8 ms, which failed `sound_ranges_frames_at_144`) not repeated by the interleaved runs (1.6 and 2.3 ms). At 1280 by 800 (`FRAME_SCREEN`, which the T0 tree lacks: compared with T14's logs alone) nothing is slower by 0.3 ms than T14's review; the palettes under the wheel (with the review's 0.12 s rest before a row's picture) 0.30 ms each, the area with the Palette tab in front 2.66. `editor_perf` once, alone at a load of 2.5 (its interleaved runs fell at a load of 9 to 12 and are not counted): every per-frame line within 0.2 ms of the T0 log. Over the budget in today's logs, as on the T0 tree: area084's overview and turning views under the wheel (7.8 to 9.5 ms) and its edit frame (10.7 to 11.3 ms, the T0 tree's 10.9 to 12.0); area092's edit frame at the line (6.6 to 7.1 ms, the T0 tree's 6.8 to 7.7). **The icon mesh cache: not built**, by the plan's rule (line 782 has the numbers: the interface views +0.025 ms, tessellation +1.6%). **Built.** (1) **The sky under a high camera** (the khaki bands of T14's 2559 by 800 pictures): older than the redesign (skyboxes since 2026-10-01) and outside the shell. The skybox stood at ground level around the camera, as the client draws it for its cameras (25 m up at most); an area seen whole puts the view's camera 151 m up (Chapter1's `map_m1q1a`), and the sides of a view four times as wide as high look 57° off straight down, onto the horizon fade's upper wall, all in the area's fog color (that area's sun fog is the khaki). `mg_render::renderer::sky_place` raises the sky with a camera higher than the game's highest (`GAME_CAMERA_HIGHEST`, 25 m) to stay that far under it: unchanged for the game's cameras, and from high up the sky as the game's highest sees it. What remains at the very sides, the fade's dark lower band over the sky's own forest (about 45% of the fog color where it was all of it), is what the geometry gives the game's highest camera at that angle (seen with the fade switched off); the 25 m is the wiki's CameraMaxDist and the look from it is inferred, not compared in the client. Pitched from high up, the change shows too: from 125 m looking 30° down, the view's top, 10° under the horizon, showed the sky's clouds and now shows the fade, as a camera looking that far down does in the game (pictures before and after, the Tile Border on and off, in `target/ui-mock/impl/m3-t15/sky/`). (2) **The Left Dock's tabs read "Module" and "Resources" at its first 260 px**, as the Main board: they were 2.4 points too wide beside the Module panel's four buttons and the fold button T14 put on the bar, and turned to icons. A dock's tabs now close their words up (`mg_uikit::shell::DOCK_TAB_TIGHT_PADDING`, an icon tab's 8 px) before they give them up; the kit's `DockTabs` gains the middle form and `tabs_width_in` (the Bottom Dock's tools are laid out by it). (3) **The README's four pictures and the manual's described table** made again from this branch (the harness 1022 high, the Bottom Dock 46 points higher than M2's log; the agdb note's recipe otherwise as it was). (4) `doc_tabs_crowded`, a screens case of a crowded bar. **For the owner: where a cut title is cut** (`target/ui-mock/impl/m3-t15/tabcut/`): today's end cut ("at_m1q4…" on every tab in sight), a middle cut keeping the extension ("at…01.nss") and one dropping it ("at_…001"); Chapter1's `at_m1q4c_m1q_001` and `at_m1q4e_m1q_001` still read alike cut in the middle, what tells them apart lying in the middle of the name. Either cut is about two hours (the title's prefix and suffix measured in `mg_uikit::dock::doc_tab_title` and kept in the title cache, its tests and the manual's line); unbuilt until ruled. **Docs**: the Where Things Moved rows M3 reached ticked (the Floating Editors' main window behind and their New line, the Welcome page's Left Dock and disabled buttons, the Top Bar's Undo and Redo acting on the 2DA, talk table, tileset and hak editors (their own buttons go with M4's headers), the 1280 window's menus and file buttons, the script's dot, the blueprint as a tab); Part 4's rows settled where M3 settles them; the deferred list 233 to 231 (two entries closed, two joined into one whose issue 21 still waits on a drag by hand, one renamed for Quick Open; `docs/log.md` names each and its reason); the manual and `docs/parity/checklists.md` swept (each task had kept them; the Left Dock's tab words and the sky changed here). **Handed to T15 and answered**: the old tests' `docks_fold_by_width` was settled by T14's second review (gone, the yields on in every harness); the editor area's margin by T14 (flush); T13's line, the Outliner's rows and the tree's sharing `contents_ui`, is so since T13 (whether the tree keeps its areas opened out is M4's, R2); `vendor/egui_dock` waits on upstream offering its two additions. **Inherits** (M4, by T15's call, not a ruling): the Right Dock's columns' seam stays a line, not dragged: the columns change with the Properties column (S2), which puts the Preview back among the first column's tabs; the crowded bar's cut waits on the owner's ruling. The renderer change (the sky's place) should pass CI on a temporary branch before the merge, as renderer changes do.

**Review, 2026-11-14.** An independent review of T15 (its commit `1b1fec61`) found seven faults; each is fixed, each new test seen to fail with the behavior broken in the source (the failure text in brackets). (1) **The sky's height was measured from world z 0**: the game's 25 m is its camera's reach from its focus (CameraMaxDist), not a height, so a game camera over a raised tile had its sky moved. `mg_render::renderer::sky_place` takes the camera now and raises the sky by what the eye stands higher over its target than the game's camera at its farthest would at the same pitch (`shadow::GAME_CAMERA_REACH`, the height rule of `camera_fade_point`): exactly at ground level for every camera within 25 m of its target, over any tile, at any pitch; `GAME_CAMERA_HIGHEST` is gone. `renderer::tests::the_sky_stands_under_the_camera_as_the_game_s` holds game cameras over a 10 m tile at 30° and 89° and far cameras at the game's height over the sky [T15's rule: "[30, 40, 10] 25 m 89°: left: … 9.996193, right: … 0.0"; the raise halved: "151 m 89.9°: 97.99986 over the sky"]. (2) **The client**: `client_render.rs`'s `sky_under_the_game_s_farthest_camera_look` (ttr01, the Grass_Clear sky, a magenta fog, the player at the field's west edge) took the client at `SetCameraFacing(90, 25, 1)` and `(90, 25, 60)`, its farthest; Moonglow's camera fitted on the ground alone is 23 m at pitch 2 and 23.5 m at pitch 60, both within the reach, and Moonglow's pictures there are the same before and after to the pixel. The sky beside the field is 7.9 of 255 from the client's at that fitted camera (its rows of the fade up to about 20 off); a degree less pitch, within what the fit can tell on a field of grass (fov 48 and focus 1.25 taken as fitted for the reference scenes, so about a degree and a meter or two), gives 6.1, the fade's rows within about 5 of the client's, and the ground fits about as well; either camera is within the reach, so the rule moves neither. And at 30° down the view's top is the fade's wall in the fog color in the client as in Moonglow, the side effect the review raised. A first fit over the whole picture said 32 m (beyond the reach): it was fitting the sky, and moved to 23.5 once the sky moved; fitted on the ground it holds. The client's window is 1024 by 768, so no wide client picture; beyond its reach, Moonglow's view from 150 m at the same pitches stands as high over the sky as the game's farthest camera (the agdb note argues it). Seen and not about the sky's place: the client tints the last meters of ground at the field's far edge toward the fog color: its own fog, always on from 30 m before the clip distance ([the tile border's notes](research/notes_tile_border.md)), which Moonglow's picture, drawn with Fog off, leaves out. Client PIDs 581180, 581487, 595030, 595239, each gone when its picture was taken. Pictures in `target/ui-mock/impl/m3-t15-fix/sky/` (`*_client`, `*_moonglow_before`/`_after`, `*_far_before`/`_after`, `*_far_wide_*`). (3) **The picture guard held one side**: `drawing::the_sky_under_a_camera_higher_than_the_game_s` passed with the sky at the camera. Measured, the side strips are 44% of the fog color's sum under the fix, 27% with the sky at the camera, 35% 12 m under it, 99% 100 m under it; held between 39% and 50% ["… (44% of the fog color), not 27%" at the camera; "not 99%" 100 m under it]. (4) **README pictures**: Module Properties' OK and Cancel showed under the floating windows; `readme_screenshots` closes it after the module opens (the editor area behind shows its No Area Open tab), and the four pictures are made again by the recipe; the manual's described table is taken with the log closed (its first line showed cut at the window's left). The recipe's note is superseded by one that says both. (5) **Where a tight tab's words are painted**: kit `a_dock_tab_s_words_stand_by_its_padding_in_each_form` reads the words' place in each form, swept, every form seen ["260: 10 at the left, 6 at the right" with `paint_dock_tab` taking `DOCK_TAB_PADDING` in the tight form]. (6) The Left Dock's comment says the three forms; `DockTabs::form_in` says tabs of which one has no icon keep the closer words where nothing fits. (7) **Docs**: `docs/parity/checklists.md`'s sky row, the manual's sky (04) and the dock tabs' tight words (02), and the Done paragraph's load (sampled at each run's start; `ab2/loads.txt` ends at 9.61; `ab1/` gave +4.4% tessellation; the decision stands, under the plan's 25% either way).

**The owner's ruling, 2026-11-14:** "Tab cut in the middle to keep." Built: a document tab whose name does not fit is cut in the middle and keeps its extension (`at…01.nss`), in `mg_uikit::dock::doc_tab_title` (`middle_cut`), kept in T9's title cache as before: as much of the name's start and end as fits, the ellipsis between them, the extension (after the last dot, one to eight letters or digits) whole wherever a character of the name fits beside it, else the whole name cut in the middle; a name with no extension is cut in the middle all the same, a name of one character never; the whole name stays in the tab's tip and its accessible name. Every tab bar's titles, the main area's groups and the floating windows' panes alike, are laid out by `tabs::prepare` through it, and egui_dock cuts nothing itself: one place. Tests: `dock::tests::a_name_too_wide_is_cut_in_the_middle_keeping_its_extension` (short, exact fit, long, no extension, one character, the ellipsis alone), `a_cut_name_fits_its_room_in_egui_s_fonts` (letters, wide letters and emoji in the button font), app `doc_tabs::a_crowded_bar_shows_both_ends_of_a_name_it_cuts` ["its end and extension: long_na…" with the old end cut; "long….nss" with the extension not kept]. Pictures: `target/ui-mock/impl/m3-t15-fix/dark/doc_tabs_crowded.png` and `light/`; the manual's line in chapter 2. Cut by characters, as egui lays out a glyph a character: an emoji sequence joined by zero-width joiners can be parted.

#### Part 5. Decided

Decided by the implementer on 2026-11-11:

- **U1.** Wide layout from W ≥ 2560: Left Dock 320 by default, Right Dock two columns when wider than 560.
- **U2.** Replace README's `area.jpg` at M3c (the shell picture changes then); the editors' screenshots at M5.
- **U3.** Each dock keeps one fold control (issue 10's rule): for Left and Right, open, a button at the end of the dock's tab bar (the Left Dock's at its bar's end toward the editor area, after the Module panel's buttons; the Right Dock's at its bar's end by the window's edge, after its tabs, and in the wide layout's two columns at the end of the last column's bar), and folded, the chevron at the strip's top (T14's review and second review); the Status Bar switch for Bottom.
- **U4.** The Status Bar shows the module's name and "(modified)", the path in the tip.
- **U5.** Existing settings keep the Log open; new installations start closed.
- **U6.** No dragging panels between docks in M3 (ruled by the owner as R5).
- **U7.** Ctrl+Shift+P opens Quick Open restricted to commands.
- **O5.** `mg-uikit` takes egui_dock 0.21 behind an optional `dock` feature, so that the viewer gets the dock style and tab marks from the same place: ruling 9 put the shell in the shared crate. The crate's "egui alone" comment is changed with it.
- **Merge points.** M3 is merged into `develop` at three points, M3a, M3b and M3c, each coherent with the manual true.

#### Part 6. Rulings for M3

Put to the owner on 2026-11-11 with a recommendation each, and ruled the same day: "You may proceed as suggested." Each is carried out as its recommendation says.

- **R1 (the plan's O1). Escape on a docked tab with OK and Cancel** (a Properties tab, Options). Today Escape cancels only a floating one (`tabs.rs:393`), so As Tabs takes it away from new users, and the Options board's hints omit it (proposal finding 13.4). **Ruled, as recommended:** Escape cancels the active docked tab that has OK and Cancel while the pointer or keyboard is in it, as floating does; the hints then show Esc. Gates T10.
- **R2 (O2). The module tree's area rows opened out to their objects, once the Outliner exists.** The boards' Module panel lists areas without arrows; the moves tables do not say. **Ruled, as recommended:** keep both in M3 (builders rely on "the tree follows the selected object", and its tests are many); decide the removal with M4's area editor. Gates T13. *Note, 2026-11-14:* the following moved to the Outliner by the owner's ruling in T13's review ("Yes: while the Outliner shows, the tree stops following the selection"): the tree keeps its areas opened out when the builder opens them, and follows the selection only while no Outliner shows.
- **R3 (O3). Plugin panels** (`Tab::PluginPanel`, Plugins › Panels) as editor tabs or windows, following the switch, or as Right Dock panels. **Ruled, as recommended:** tabs or windows in M3 (they are not one editor's); revisit when the dialogs become panels in M4. Gates T10.
- **R4 (O4). Retiring "Toolbar buttons" at M4, not M3**, since the area toolbar's Icons-only mode is what keeps it on one row until M4's header. **Ruled, as recommended:** yes, at M4. The Top Bar ignores the setting from T2 and the Options control is renamed until then. Gates T2's Options wording only.
- **R5 (new, from U6 with T4 and T5). Panels are fixed to their docks in M3.** Today the resource browser and the palettes can be dragged to a side or into the middle and are docked there (GitHub issues 10, 11, 20 and 23, the `docs/deferred.md` entries named in T4 and T5); with the docks as panels that goes away: Resources lives in the Left Dock, Palette and Preview in the Right Dock, and a dock folds but its panels do not move. **Ruled, as recommended:** accept it for M3 and M4 (the mock's shell places every panel, and no board shows a panel moved), and file moving panels between docks as its own later piece of work only if the owner wants it. Gates T4 and T5; tasks before them (T0 to T3) do not depend on it.

#### Part 7. Scope moves

| Move | Reason |
|---|---|
| **S1** "Toolbar buttons" retirement, M3 to M4 | R4 |
| **S2** The Properties panel (a placed object's properties in the Right Dock, apply-at-once with Undo, ruling 2), M3 to M4's area editor | It changes the editor's insides (its pages as a rail, Cancel becoming Undo) against M3's exit "without yet changing their insides" |
| **S3** Problems and Search Results switches, and every editor's own dock panels (Reference, Node, Cell…), stay in M4 | They come with their panels |
| **S4** The area view's hint line moves *into* M3 (T12) | Rule 5 and the Main board put hints in the Status Bar, and a hints slot with no client would ship empty |
| **S5** The area's Outliner moves into M3 (T13); the other editors' Outliners stay M4 | The M3 bullet names it, and an Outliner frame with no contents would show an empty panel |

#### Handing out the tasks

A standing note for whoever hands out the tasks:

- One code writer at a time in the worktree.
- After each task, an independent read-only reviewer on the commit, then a fix round.
- The owner's findings from live testing M2 take the writer's slot ahead of M3 tasks.
- The hand-run timing tests (`frame_perf`, `editor_perf`, `world_perf`, `campaign_perf`, `nui_perf`) are run after any task that renames or moves controls, since the suite only compiles them.

#### Critical files for implementation

All under `crates/` in the worktree:

- `crates/mg-ui/src/lib.rs`
- `crates/mg-ui/src/tabs.rs`
- `crates/mg-ui/src/commands.rs`
- `crates/mg-ui/src/settings.rs`
- `crates/mg-ui/src/open_tabs.rs`
- `crates/mg-uikit/src/controls.rs`
- `crates/mg-ui/tests/app/windows.rs`

### M4. Editor by editor

- (from T12) Each editor's change gives the Status Bar its board's hints and counts through `shell::shown` (keys by `shell::HintKeys`, a command's as bound): the blueprints' 3D view (the Creature boards), the script's line and column count, the conversation editor's hints and word count, the tables', NUI's, the tileset's, the hak's, the journal's, the factions', the galleries' and the Resources board's counts at the bar's right (T12's Done paragraph lists them).

- (from T14) The narrow and wide boards' editor parts: Narrow.png's compact area header (E < 990: Show "5 of 10", the moon, the icon popovers), the tool strip at the view's left and the Properties tab beside Palette and Preview; WideArea's header unfolded into its row and its Properties column (the wide layout's second column holds the Preview until then); the palette's narrow row (the types' dropdown with Standard and Custom beside it, Expand All and Collapse All at the Group By row's end) and GalleryWide's 480 Gallery column; Problems and Search Results switches as icons under 1400 with the Log's.

- (from T9's review, the owner's word) With two editor groups the Right Dock goes while a script's group has the focus and comes back with the area's: the active editor fills the docks, and a script brings no panel there until the script editor has its own (Reference) in the Right Dock in M4.

Known differences left by the theme pass (M2), which the editors' own changes settle:

- ~~An open combo box fills `bar`; the mock's `.combo.open` is `raised-hover`.~~ Settled by M4's U3: every combo box of the application (`widgets::dropdown`, the choice lists) is drawn open in `raised-hover` with its `accent` ring (`mg_uikit::combo_style`, given to the `Ui` it is laid out in); the theme's `widgets.open` keeps `bar`, which egui fills the window on top's title bar with.
- The slider's thumb at rest is the rail's color (egui fills both with one color); the mock's is `text-2`.
- A disabled control fades whole (egui's opacity), but the check box and radio, which have disabled tokens of their own.
- In small script windows the Compiler and Help strip crowds the code (the script editor's change).
- ~~With Icons and text, the area toolbar takes one row more than before the drawn icons at some widths: its buttons keep 4 px between icon and words, not the mock's 6, and are a point or two wider each than with their glyphs (the area editor's header and popovers settle it).~~ Settled by M4's U3: the toolbar is the header's one row, the kit's buttons 6 px between icon and words.
- A number field's arrows are 22 px square, an icon button's size, beside the field (the forms' own changes size them with the field).
- In the NUI designer's narrow layout (under 760 px), opening a combo box inside the Properties menu closes the menu: egui keeps one popup open at a time. It was so before the redesign, when any click in that menu closed it (the kit's form menus now stay open for a click inside); the designer's own change gives the narrow layout its Properties as a panel, not a menu.
- A color picker's hex field is cut short under a window's larger text (a fixed 70 px). No color button opens where the larger text applies today (the conversation editor), so only a test shows it; the color picker's own change sizes the field by its text.
- ~~With the Palettes pane dragged to about 135 px, a blueprint type's list toolbar (Expand All, List) spills left under the Fold Palettes button. Seen in the icons' fix round and not checked against the code before the redesign; the Right Dock's least width (M3) and the palette's own change settle it, and `views_draw_nothing_over_anything_else` is to exercise that state again then.~~ Settled by M3's T5: the palettes are the Right Dock's panel, at least 280 px wide, its fold button beside its edge outside it; `views_draw_nothing_over_anything_else` looks at the dock at 280, and `the_palette_fits_the_right_dock_from_its_least_width_to_600` sweeps every palette type from 280 to 600.

- The Right Dock's Preview panel (M3's T6) shows the model viewer's toolbar as the window did, wrapped to three rows at 320 px (about 95 points over the 3D view). The blueprints' change gives it the boards' one header row over the 3D view (the moves table's "Preview window: info line, Animation, Pause, Frame, Lights, Shadow, Shaders, Export Files…" row: about 60 points back to the view) and the Creature board's info line in its tab bar. The Properties panel stacked under the Palette and Preview tabs is the area editor's (S2).

**Planned in detail, 2026-11-14.**

M4 is the largest milestone of the redesign: every editor's insides change, and with them the docks learn to hold what each editor brings. Cut into tasks of one to two days of an agent's work it is 31 tasks, U0 to U30 (U: T is M3's), about 57 days of work, three and a half times M3's 16. About 500 test lines migrate (the area toolbar's lookups, about 110 lines through `common::area_tool`; the blueprint pages clicked by name, about 130 lines, through one new helper; the 25 lines that set "Toolbar buttons" or the area's hidden tools; Tile Properties' OK and Cancel; the script's and the conversation's panes where a test runs As Tabs), and about as many are added. The owner's word of 2026-11-14 holds: M4 lands on `ui-redesign` task by task and goes into `develop` once, at its end, after asking. Seven facts frame the plan:

- **Tests run floating, and a floating editor keeps its panels.** `Settings::default()` is As Floating Windows (M3, Part 2, decision 4), and a floating editor draws its own side and bottom panes inside its window (decision 3; the Floating board draws exactly that: the script's Reference and Help at the window's right, Compiler, Bookmarks and Search Results under its text). So each panel's body is one function with two hosts, the dock when the editor is docked and in front, the editor's own pane when it floats, and most of the roughly 1,344 UI tests keep passing as they are; each task adds As Tabs tests for the docks.
- **An editor's state already lives outside its window.** The views are kept on `Moonglow` by document (`scripts`, lib.rs:681; `dialog_views`, :730; `model_views`, :893; `area_views`, :904), so a dock panel reads the active editor's state by its key. The docks are drawn before the editor area: a panel shows what the editor left the frame before (as the Status Bar has since T12), and a panel that draws a 3D view the editor may also draw is drawn late, in the room the dock kept (the Preview's `preview_slot`, area_tools.rs:786-800).
- **A placed object's Properties already apply at once.** `blueprint::ok_cancel`'s contract (blueprint/mod.rs:320-327): "what was changed is the module's already"; OK closes, Cancel sets back the window's changes as one Undo step. The Properties panel (S2, ruling 2) is therefore `edit_many`'s body without its OK and Cancel, not a new way of editing.
- **The kit has none of M4's parts yet.** `mg-uikit` has the metrics (`EDITOR_HEADER` 30, metrics.rs:32; `TOOL_SHELF` 36, :36; `TOOL_BUTTON` 28, :38; `POPOVER_RADIUS`, :53) but no header row, popover, More, tool shelf, page list, footer row, step list or gallery cell. Its `shell::Yields` already carries the area header's three yields (`header_compact`, `value_text`, `into_more`), which nothing in `mg-ui` reads yet. What it has and M4 reuses: `segmented` (controls.rs:136), `Join` (icons.rs:347), `IconButton::menu`, `PanelHeader` (shell.rs:951), `DockTabs`, `TreeGroup::eye` (tree.rs:440), `TreeRow::second` (:573), `KeyCap`, `Badge`, `Hints`, `StatusSwitch`.
- **The order has one constraint the paragraph below does not show.** The Problems and Search Results panels come, by the paragraph, with "the dialogs that become panels" at the end; but the script editor's Search Results (U13) and the tileset editor's Check (U20) need them earlier. So the Bottom Dock learns to hold several panels, each with its Status Bar switch, in U2, and each of the two panels arrives with its first feeder (no switch without a panel, M3's S3); the dialogs fill them in U28.
- **"Toolbar buttons" goes when its last reader does (R4).** Six toolbars read it (`icons::Tools::of` at script_view.rs:755, dialog_view.rs:1095, model_view.rs:1229, nui_view.rs:876, hak_view.rs:733; `Tools::of_area` at area_view.rs:3092). Each editor's task stops reading it for its own toolbar; the setting, its Options control and its draft go in U23, after the hak editor (U21), the last of them, and with the Options page that the board draws without it.
- **Code is read with agdb** (`trail` is retired). Every task ends as M3's did: `cargo test --workspace --exclude mg-corpus-tests --lib --bins --tests`, the three clippy commands, `cargo fmt`, where docs changed `okf index docs`, `okf lint docs --links` and a `docs/log.md` entry, an agdb note for what the code cannot tell, the task's pictures rendered with `screens.rs` before and after in both themes (`MG_LIGHT=1`) into `target/ui-mock/impl/m4-uN/`, and the hand-run timing tests after any task that renames or moves controls (the suite only compiles them).

Four merge points, review and stability points on the branch (none goes into `develop`), each coherent with the manual true and the task's moves rows ticked:

- **M4a, after U8**: the area editor as the Main, Narrow, WideArea, AreaPaint, AreaTiles, Floating and GizmoStates boards draw it (header, popovers, tool shelf, compass, Paint and Tiles states, Tile Properties and Properties panels, the Right Dock's sections, the handles).
- **M4b, after U15**: blueprints (page list, columns, Preview panel), the script editor and the conversation editor, each with its panels docked and floating.
- **M4c, after U24**: the tables and the rest (NUI, tileset, hak, journal, factions, the read-only resource, Options, Welcome); "Toolbar buttons" retired.
- **M4d, after U30**: the shared gallery cell, the pickers, the dialog pattern, the dialogs that become panels, the closing pass; then the owner is asked to merge into `develop`.

#### Part 1. How today's editors are built (what the tasks change)

Checked against the code on 2026-11-14. "OK and Cancel" below is `blueprint::ok_cancel` (blueprint/mod.rs:328) or `side_doc::ok_cancel` (side_doc.rs:157), both drawing `ok_cancel_saying` (blueprint/mod.rs:338-368): a bottom panel, OK then Cancel at its left, Close alone when read-only. No editor draws them at a header's right end today.

**The area editor.**

- The toolbar is `area_view::toolbar` (area_view.rs:3088-3434, called at :3004), one `horizontal_wrapped` (:3104) named "Area toolbar" (:3107). In order: a "Show X" toggle per kind (:3109-3120), Start, All, None; Night, Lighting, Shadows (`shadows_button`, :4498), Fog, Fade Geometry (`lead_dropdown`, :4334), Animations, Grass, Tile Border, Shaders, Grid, Minimap, Play Sounds, Sound Ranges, Ambient, Music; Snap and Turn dropdowns, Together, Onto Objects; Walkmesh… (`walkmesh_button`, :4436, a menu); Select Tiles and Paint (disabled read-only); Area Properties; Reorient Camera (:3381, inline, not a command); Go to Start Location (:3386; also `Id::GoToStart`); To Scratch; "Save N as Prefab…" (several selected); last the warnings "problems" and "N missing" (:3420-3436). Paint's second strip follows it while Paint is on (`paint_mode::strip`, paint_mode.rs:1011-1104: Scatter, Row, Fill, Erase; Radius, Density, Count All, Spacing, By Size, Flow, Turn, Scale, Gap, Height, Lock by mode; "N chosen").
- The strip's right-click menu (:3098-3103) is `toolbar_menu` (:4381-4433, also on Options › Area, options.rs:1001), which writes `Settings::hidden_area_tools` (settings.rs:820); `Tools::of_area` (icons.rs:248) folds it with `toolbar_icons_only` into a mask over `AREA_TOOLS` (icons.rs:181, 29 names).
- There is no Terrain mode and no compass. Terrain brushes are the tiles palette's (`terrain_mode::palette_ui`, terrain_mode.rs:1621-1800); the minimap is drawn at the view's top right (`minimap_overlay`, area_view.rs:3878-3935, placed at :3861), where the boards put the compass (the minimap goes to the bottom right).
- Select Tiles' commands are its right-click menu (`tile_select::context_menu`, tile_select.rs:521-572) and keys; no button copies, cuts or deletes tiles. Tile Properties is `Tab::TileProperties`, a tab that floats in both modes (`Tab::floats`, tabs.rs:128), drawn by `tile_select::ui` (tile_select.rs:1030-1318) with OK (tile_select.rs:1107) and Cancel and a question when closed with changes (`closing_window`, tile_select.rs:1324); its variants are drawn by `variant_picture` (tile_select.rs:1731), not the shared picture tile.
- The handles are painted in `overlays` (area_view.rs:4584-5084): spawn points (:4646-4690), rings (:4751), selection boxes (:4776-4800), the turning ring (:4839), the scale handle (:4848-4890), tilt rings (:4892), the arrows (about :4923-4960), the start location (:5022); their geometry and hit tests are `turn_rings` (:1763), `tilt_rings` (:1835), `scale_handles` (:1860). Options' "Show the turning and tilt rings…" is `no_turn_ring` (options.rs:936, read at area_view.rs:3495).
- A placed object's Properties: a double click (area_view.rs:5553-5559) or the context menu calls `open_properties` (:6637), which opens `Tab::Instance` (tabs.rs:71), drawn by `blueprint::edit_many` (blueprint/mod.rs:2007): OK and Cancel first (:2050), then the pages as a wrapped row of selectables (:2062-2066; `instance_pages`, :1994).
- The palette is `palette_view::ui` (palette_view.rs:1109-1946): the types' strip (:1214-1244; a dropdown under 300 points, `TYPE_COMBO_UNDER`, :1039), Standard and Custom (:1324-1336), the search, the fold row (Expand All, Collapse All, List, Gallery, New…, More, :1420-1466), Group by (:1535-1544), the gallery's slider (`appearance_gallery::size_slider`, appearance_gallery.rs:202, called at palette_view.rs:1508).
- Docks: `editor_brings_outliner` (shell.rs:264) and `editor_brings_palette` (:274), read by `Panel::available` (:231); the Right Dock (`right_dock`, :1778) draws Palette and Preview as one tab bar, or in the wide layout as two columns with the Preview in the second (:1871-1910). Status Bar: `area_view/bar.rs` (`status`, :25, the states' hints, the counts, `selection_counts`).
- Tests: `common::area_tool` (common.rs:644, a button inside "Area toolbar") is used 58 times (area_view 19, tiles 13, areas 7, drawing 6, outliner, tree and options_and_keys 3 each), and about 110 lines name a toolbar label; `in_toolbar` (:616) and `apart` (:631) leave the group out; 25 lines set `toolbar_icons_only`, `hidden_area_tools` or `hidden_in_areas` (icon_tips.rs, options_and_keys.rs:783-926, areas.rs:400). Pictures: `area_toolbar_narrow` (screens.rs:5245), `toolbar_icons_only` (:5297), the `area_*` and `tile_*` cases, the `shell_*` family. `frame_perf` opens areas by action and clicks by label (frame_perf.rs:620, :629).

**Blueprints, Module Properties and Area Properties.**

- Each kind has a `PAGES` constant (creature.rs:23, 14 pages; item 7, placeable 10, door 10, trigger 8, encounter 6, sound 5, store 6, waypoint 5), `pages()` (blueprint/mod.rs:51) chooses by type, a page is drawn by its name, and the chosen page is `app.blueprint_pages` (:68). Module Properties has an enum (`module_props::Page`, module_props.rs:35, six pages; a plain row, :104-108; OK and Cancel, :85); Area Properties a constant (area_props.rs:19, eight; six shared when several are edited, :29; the row :132-136; OK and Cancel :44).

Its toolbar is the model view's (`model_view::show`, model_view.rs:1125; one `horizontal_wrapped`, :1177-1280: the info line, Animation, Play or Pause, Frame, Lights, Shadow, Shaders, Pop Out, Export Files…; `Tools::of`, :1229), three rows at 320 points. The Appearance page's own preview is `situated::inline_preview` (blueprint/situated.rs:1270; creature.rs:475, item.rs:803), with Pop Out and Bring Back.
- Tests: `blueprint_harness` (common.rs:424-444) and `field` (:476); about 130 lines click a page by its name (blueprints 60, fields 25, inventories 10, options_and_keys 7, area_view 6, the rest one or two), and many assume one page drawn at a time. The Status Bar gives a tab with OK and Cancel Esc, Tab and Shift + Tab (`shell::shown`, shell.rs:1329-1346).

The line's count reads `text` (:885), the line's own text, not `line_text` (:893), which falls back to the talk table: proposal finding 13.2 confirmed (:1414-1418). No hints (`shown`'s default). The Top Bar's own icons-only use is `Tools::icons_alone` (shell.rs:560, 644, 700), which stays.

**Moonglow Viewer** pins 15 toolset crates at `v3.14.0` and neither `mg-ui` nor `mg-uikit` (its `Cargo.toml`); nothing M4 changes reaches it. M4's kit parts are the API M6 adopts.

#### Part 2. Architecture decisions for M4

1. **The kit's new parts (`mg-uikit`, add-only, egui alone).** Each lands with its first user, as M1 had it ("helpers come with the milestones that first use them"), and none names a type of `mg-ui`, so that the viewer can take it at M6.
   - `header.rs` (U1): `EditorHeader`, 30 points: a left group (what you do), a spacer, a right group (how the view looks), an end slot (OK and Cancel, or Close). It lays itself out against the width it has (section 6.1): first the left group's word buttons lose their words, then they move into a **More** popover, secondary commands before Save; the end slot never yields and is never covered. It records where each control went (on the row, as an icon, in More), so that every editor's test can narrow it to its least width and find each control.
   - `popover.rs` (U1): `Popover`, hung below or below-right of its button (never over the view's top-right overlays), radius 4, `--shadow`; a title with an optional master switch, sub headings, check rows, separators; `Popover::unfolded` lays the same rows into the header as toggles where the row has room (section 5.3).
   - `shelf.rs` (U1): `ToolShelf`, 36 points, 28-point buttons, on, hover, disabled, separators, one accessible group named "Tools".
   - The view's overlays (U4): the compass painter (the light board's `cp-*` tokens) and the view's corner buttons; the readout chip (U8; theme-aware, section 11.5).
   - `pages.rs` (U6 and U9): `PageRail` (a panel's pages as icons at its left edge, the Properties panel's) and `PageList` (a form's pages at its left, with icons, marking the pages in view) with `PageBar` (the bar over each page on the one scroll).
   - `form.rs` (U10): columns of 440 to 520 points for forms.
   - `dialog.rs` (U26 and U27): `Footer` (46 points, a rule above, a setting or count at the left, buttons at the right, the main one first in `accent-fill`), the body's 12 points, `StepList` (a check on done steps) and the wizard's page-header bar; `EmptyState` (U24).
   - `gallery.rs` (U25): `GalleryCell` (Quiet Tiles, section 8.4: rim, name rule, detail line, states, the kind's icon while making, "No model" badge, favorite mark, the small variant), `GalleryGrid` (cells that grow, and fixed cells in justified rows) and the one size slider, 64 to 220 (`appearance_gallery.rs:198-199` already clamps to it).
   - Three known differences of M2 are the kit's to settle where its parts draw them: the open combo box in `raised-hover` (U3), a disabled kit control drawn in its own tokens rather than faded whole (U1), the slider's thumb in `text-2` (U25).
2. **The docks hold what the active editor brings.** M3's registry is generalized in U2, before any editor needs it:
   - `Dock` gains `Bottom`. A dock holds **sections** (up to three stacked in the Right Dock, as the Talk, Factions and NUI boards draw them), each a tab bar of one or more panels. The Main board's Right Dock is two sections (Palette and Preview; Properties), the Conversation board's two (Node, Data, Search; Conversation), the Script board's two (Reference; Help).
   - `editor_brings(app)` replaces `editor_brings_palette` and `editor_brings_outliner`: by the active docked editor's kind it gives the Left Dock's Outliner contents, the Right Dock's sections and the Bottom Dock's own panels (a script's Compiler and Bookmarks, the NUI designer's Checks). The global panels (Log, Problems, Search Results) stay whatever the editor.
   - Under 1400 points the sections fold into one tab bar (section 5.2; Narrow draws Palette, Properties, Preview), in the wide layout the first section takes the first column and the rest the second (WideArea: Palette and Preview, then Properties; the Preview goes back to the first column, as T14 left it to do).
   - A panel's body is one function taking the editor's tab; the dock calls it when the editor is docked and in front, the editor's own pane when it floats. A body that draws a 3D view the editor may also draw uses the late slot, generalized from `preview_slot`.
   - Which tab of a section is in front and the sections' shares are kept per editor kind in `Settings` (as `right_dock_width` is), not per document.
   - The Bottom Dock gets N tabs and the Status Bar N switches, each with its count badge (Problems' errors, Search Results' hits, Checks'), icons alone under 1400 (`Yields::status_switch_icons`), the switches' width arithmetic (shell.rs:916-930) made general.
   - A panel exists only once its feeder is built: **no merge point shows a panel nothing can fill** (M3's rule kept). From then on Problems and Search Results show their switches as every board draws them, with no module open too (Welcome.png), their body an empty state saying what fills it.
   - The Plugins menu's "pane last clicked" (`plugins.pane`) stays as T13 left it, no Outliner counting; a panel an editor brings counts as that editor's pane (decided in U2 and written into 17-plugin-api.md).
   - Why now: the boards draw every editor this way, and doing it once keeps each editor's task to its own panels.
3. **Each editor's Outliner** (S5): the Left Dock's Outliner shows, by the active editor, an area's objects (T13), a conversation's Bookmarks (U15), a 2DA's columns with their eyes (U16), the NUI window's layers (U18), a tileset's parts (U20), a hak's contents by type (U21), the factions (U22). One `outliner::contents` chooses; the kit's `TreeGroup::eye` and `TreeRow::second` draw them.
4. **OK and Cancel move into the header's end slot** by one change to `ok_cancel_saying` (an `in_header` form, the same tips and `Pressed`), taken up by each editor's own task, so that the 13 callers move one at a time; Options' own pair (options.rs:701-727) the same in U23. Escape (R1) keeps reading where the buttons are drawn (`cancel_rects`). GitHub issue 38's fix holds with them: a tab that pins its buttons and scrolls its own page fits its pane (`TabViewer::scroll_bars` gives `[false, false]`), and `a_properties_window_s_buttons_stay_put` runs at 1.0, 1.25 and 1.5 for every editor moved (the agdb note "Windows with OK and Cancel at their foot fit their pane").
5. **The dialogs that become panels** (ruling 5): Find Instance and the module-wide Find and Replace Text's hits go to Search Results, the search's form as the panel's header; Build Module's results to Problems (Q4; Verify's findings, Q5); Hak Pak Conflict Analysis, Resources Used and Claims Table become Bottom Dock tabs opened by their commands; Edit Areas Together, Light Maker and Bake Walkmesh become sections of the area editor's Right Dock while open; Plugins becomes a tab. Their `Moonglow` fields (`find_instance`, lib.rs:915; `text_replace`, :1026; `build`, :919; `hak_report`, :1057; `area_stats`, :929; `claims.table`; `area_chooser`, :1024; `light_draft`, :908; `bake.draft`, :911; `plugins.window`, :1341) become the panels' state, and their commands show the panel. Areas Not Yours is a question asked when saving (claims.rs:851), so it stays a dialog with the confirmations.
6. **Settings and their migrations.** `Settings::default()` keeps its meaning; nothing becomes As Tabs for an existing builder.
   - `toolbar_icons_only` is retired in U23 (R4): the field, its draft and the Options control go; an old file still reads, the value dropped silently, nothing unread and no copy put aside (the precedent test at settings.rs:2000-2017); an older Moonglow reading a newer file takes its default, Icons and text, which only its own toolbars read. `icons::Tools` keeps its Top Bar use (`icons_alone`) and loses the rest.
   - `hidden_area_tools` (settings.rs:820) stays, with its 29 names: a name hides the control from the header row, from its popover and from the wide layout's unfolded row; the header's right-click menu and Options › Area edit it (the moves row "The strip's right-click menu (which buttons to show)"). Names are kept so stored lists keep their meaning.
   - New: the docks' per-kind sections (above). Retired, read silently: `talk_splits` with the talk table's splitter (U17).
   - Tile Properties stops being a floating tab (`Tab::floats`); an open-tabs record naming it shows the panel instead.
7. **Moonglow Viewer** is not affected by any M4 change (it takes neither crate). The kit's crate comment says the viewer uses it, which is true only from M6; U30 says so.
8. **For the owner:** Q1 to Q5 below. The rest is decided by the boards, the proposal or an existing ruling:
   - The Properties panel and Open in Tab: the Main and Floating boards draw the panel in both modes; Open in Tab opens today's tab (or window) with OK and Cancel.
   - The Terrain button of the tool shelf ("Paint terrain with the tile palette's brushes", the boards' tip): it shows the Palette on its Tiles type and is on while a terrain brush is in hand; choosing a terrain brush turns it on.
   - The area toolbar's "problems" and "N missing" (no board draws them; nothing disappears): a warning icon with its count at the right group's end, before More, its tip saying what was read.
   - Search Results is one panel (every board draws one switch), showing the last search, its form as its header.
   - Finding 13.2 is fixed in U14 (the Node panel shows that line; the board drew today's words) and finding 13.3 in U24 (the recent row is rebuilt).



#### Part 3. The plan for M4

Each task names what it builds (board by board), the moves rows it ticks (by their text), the known differences it settles, its Status Bar hints and counts (through `shell::shown`, keys by `HintKeys`), its tests (each new test seen to fail without the change), its pictures, its timing, its docs and the deferred entries it closes or must keep (by heading and line of `docs/deferred.md` today). Manual chapters by file; parity sections of `docs/parity/checklists.md` by heading. "Docked and floating" means a test in each mode (`common::Mode`).

##### M4a: The area editor and the handles (U0 to U8)

**U0. The editors' pictures and the baseline (do first; 1 day).**

- **Builds.** A `board_*` family in `crates/mg-ui/tests/screens.rs`, one case per board M4 builds, As Tabs, at the board's size, on Chapter1 (and the boards' resources: `m1q1faribeth`, `m1q3_formosa_9`, `spells.2da`, `dialog.tlk` as far as it opens, `tcn01`, a game hak, `nw_chicken.utc`, the module's journal and factions), dark and light: `board_main` (the `shell_main` it was), `board_narrow`, `board_wide`, `board_floating`, `board_area_paint`, `board_area_tiles`, `board_gizmo_states` (a dynamic placeable selected, with Shift held, and mid-drag), `board_creature`, `board_creature_wide`, `board_script`, `board_conversation`, `board_table`, `board_talk`, `board_nui`, `board_tileset`, `board_hak`, `board_resource`, `board_journal`, `board_factions`, `board_options`, `board_welcome`, `board_dialogs`, `board_pickers`, `board_gallery_palette`, `board_gallery_tiles`, `board_gallery_wide`, `board_appearance_gallery`. No `src/` change.
- **Baseline.** `frame_perf`, `editor_perf` and `nui_perf` on this tree, quiet, into `target/ui-mock/perf-m4/u0/` (compared with `perf-m3/t15/`); later tasks compare with it.
- **Coherent.** Yes.

**Done, 2026-11-14.** The 27 cases are in `screens.rs`, each `#[ignore]` and named as the list above names them; run them with `cargo test -p mg-ui --test screens -- --ignored board_ --test-threads=1` (pictures go to `screens/board/`, or to `screens-light/board/` with `MG_LIGHT=1`). They write 37 pictures, because the sheets get one picture per state: `board_gizmo_states_selected`, `_shift` and `_drag`; `board_dialogs_rename`, `_delete`, `_find_replace`, `_build`, `_area_wizard` and `_creature_wizard`; `board_pickers_portrait`, `_texture`, `_armor` and `_creature_wizard`. The sheets' windows are 1600 by 720; the rest use the board's size.

`shell_main` stays as M3 left it, with editors floating. `board_main` is the Main board As Tabs and is not an alias of it. The shared set-up is `board_window`, built on `shell_with_area`: As Tabs; the boards' tabs (Module Properties, the area with its modified dot, Faribeth, the script); the Plague Victim Pyre selected under the boards' camera (`BOARD_CAMERA`); the Right Dock on Placeables; the log closed. `area_changed`, `pyre_index` and `resources_chicken` now serve `board_tabs`, `shell_outliner` and `shell_resources_window` too. No `src/` change.

Each case's comment says what today's app shows in place of its board. The pictures are in `target/ui-mock/impl/m4-u0/` (`dark/`, `light/`), and `INDEX.md` there lists one line of differences per case.

What today's app cannot show, with the nearest state each case uses instead:

- **Panels that do not exist yet.** The header rows, popovers, tool shelf and compass are missing. So are the Properties, Node, Reference, Cell, String, Entry and Faction panels, the Outliners other than the area's, and the Bottom Dock's panels. Each case shows the editor's own panes.
- **Tile Properties** is a floating window in both modes. Its cases place it where the boards draw the panel.
- **`dialog.tlk`** cannot be opened by anything today. `board_talk` reads it as a layer of the game's, so it opens read-only; the list is not scrolled to its chosen line.
- **Missing resources.** Chapter1 has no `spells.2da`, `tcn01.set` or JUI, so the cases use scratch copies and mg-nui's `crier.jui` put into the workspace. None is saved.
- **The hak** is `data/hk/id_resources.hak`, opened over Chapter1; the board uses Neverwinter Chess.
- **`board_gizmo_states`** shows the States row only. Its object is `map_m1q1a`'s first dynamic placeable, with triggers and encounters hidden so that the drag starts on it.
- **`board_floating`** leaves out the second script tab, because a second script opens a window of its own.
- **`board_dialogs`** leaves out five dialogs that only a closing, a paste, a placed object's menu or a crash opens: Unsaved Changes, Duplicate Tags, Adjust Location, Resize Area and Recover Unsaved Work.
- **`board_pickers`**' armor piece is the game's `nw_aarcl001` in a module of its own, its window floating, as `armor_part_pictures` has it.

The Appearance and Placeable Galleries already open as tabs As Tabs.

**The baseline.** Release binaries were built once (`--no-run`). Each test then ran alone (`--ignored --nocapture --test-threads=1`) from `crates/mg-ui`, through `perf-m4/u0/run.sh`, which waits before each run for a 1-minute load under 3. The set ran twice: `frame_perf`, `frame_perf` at `FRAME_SCREEN=1280x800`, `editor_perf` and `nui_perf`, then the same again with logs prefixed `run2_`. Loads at the starts were 2.89, 2.93, 2.46 and 2.42 for the first set, and 2.21, 2.34, 2.83 and 2.92 for the second (`loads.txt`).

This tree's code is T15's, so a view counts as moved only where both runs differ from T15's quiet logs by more than 0.3 ms in the same direction (`agree.py`; against T15's interleaved `ab2` logs, `agree_ab2.txt`). The median view moved -0.020 and +0.010 ms (339 views), -0.010 and +0.040 ms at 1280 by 800 (327), and +0.08 and +0.17 ms in `editor_perf` (19).

In the first run, area092's stretch of `frame_perf` ran at about three times its time, tessellation included, so the processor was taken. That run failed its budget check (tiles palette searched as typed, wheel, 14.08 ms; all selected with the walkmesh, 11.5 to 12.3 ms). The second run did not repeat it (3.05 to 3.74 ms) and passed every test, so `run2_frame_perf.log` is the baseline.

Views moved beyond 0.3 ms in both runs, T15 then run 1 then run 2, in ms:

- **area092, wheel, at the default size.** The overview went 2.71, 7.05, 3.05; the tiles palette searched as typed 3.06, 14.08, 3.74; all selected with the walkmesh 3.13, 12.29, 3.53. Only the second run's 0.3 to 0.7 ms is in the comparison.
- **T15's own spikes, which its note names, against T15's single quiet run.** Area Properties went 3.30, 3.13 and 2.65 to about 1.63. area084's one-column Palette in front went 8.80 to 2.25 and 2.32 (idle) and 9.96 to 4.85 and 5.22 (wheel).
- **Slower, at the default size.** area084's Outliner kinds closed (wheel) went 3.73 to 4.34 in both runs.
- **Quicker, at the default size.** The resource browser (wheel) went 0.86 to 0.50. The lake with Shaders off, turning (pointer), went 2.90 to 2.17 and 2.53. Several area084 wheel views were 0.3 to 1.0 ms quicker: turning, two columns, all selected with the walkmesh.
- **At 1280 by 800, the lake's wheel and walkmesh views were 0.3 to 2.0 ms slower.** With Shaders on, all selected with the walkmesh (pointer), went 3.48 to 4.24 and 5.51.
- **`editor_perf`.** The first frame went 262.0 to 266.2 and 265.6. The frame the check as you type compiles in went 7.76 to 8.17 and 8.32.

The tree's code is unchanged, so these moves are the shared machine's.

Over the 6.94 ms budget in both runs:

- area084's overview and turning views (wheel), with Sound Ranges on and off, at 7.7 to 10.9 ms at both sizes, as at T15.
- area092's edit frame: 7.7 ms in the second run, 7.1 at T15.
- area084's edit frames: 11.1 and 12.0 ms, as at T15.

area092's overview and turning views are under budget in the second run (0.9 to 3.1 ms at the default size, up to 4.3 ms at 1280 by 800), as at T15. Nothing is newly over budget.

`nui_perf` has no T15 log, so its figures are a baseline only, first run and second run:

- a large window's idle frame: 1.00 and 0.99 ms;
- a frame after an edit of the window: 1.96 and 2.06 ms;
- a frame after an edit elsewhere: 1.00 and 1.00 ms;
- a window of text, idle: 0.74 and 0.73 ms;
- a chart of a million samples, idle: 29.8 and 38.4 ms, the one view over budget.

**U1. The kit's header, popover and tool shelf (2 days).**

- **Builds.** `EditorHeader`, `Popover`, More, `ToolShelf` (Part 2, decision 1), measured from `moonglow.css` (`.editor-header`, `.popover` with `.pop-title`, `.pop-h`, `.pop-row` and `.pop-sep`, `.shelf`); a disabled kit button in `text-off` and its tokens (known difference "a disabled control fades whole", for the kit's controls). A test helper `common::header_holds(h, group, names)`: each name on the row, as an icon, or in More.
- **User sees.** Nothing.
- **Hands on.** The header, popover and shelf to U3 and every editor after; `header_holds` to each editor's narrowing test.
- **Tests.** Kit: the yields in order (words, icons, More; the end slot never), at widths from the header's full width down to its end slot; a popover below-right never over the top-right corner; a master switch's state; the kit sheet against Foundations and LightFoundations.
- **Coherent.** Yes.

**Done, 2026-11-14.** Four new modules in `mg-uikit`, egui alone, none naming an `mg-ui` type; nothing in the application uses them yet, so nothing changes for the user.

- **`button`: `KitButton` and `ButtonLook`** (Raised `.btn`, Flat `.btn-icon` and `.btn.flat`, Toggle, Primary): an icon, words or both, a chevron where it opens something (`opens`), the open look (`raised-hover` and an `accent` ring; `open_state` without a chevron, More's `⋯`), measured before it is laid out (`width`, the same in every state). Disabled, it is drawn in the disabled tokens, not faded: `text-off` on `raised` (on nothing where flat), a toggle that is on in `accent-fill-off` and `on-fill-off` (a primary as a word button, `raised` and `text-off`: corrected in the review below, C2). Inside a disabled `Ui` the fade is taken back out and a parent's opacity kept (`controls::box_painter`, now `pub(crate)`). This settles "A disabled control fades whole" for the kit's new parts. `IconButton` and egui's own buttons still fade: changing them would change every existing toolbar now, so each editor's header settles it as it moves onto `KitButton` (U3 on).
- **`header`: `EditorHeader`, `Item`, `Place`, `Yield`, `HeaderOutput`, `places`, `more_name`.** The row is 30 px of `bar` with a `line` under it, padding 6 and gap 4: a left group, a spacer, a right group, More, a line, and the end slot (`show_with_end(ui, width, closure)`, the width the caller's, as `three_parts_sized` takes it; the closure's value comes back in `end`). Items: `button`, `words`, `icon`, `toggle(icon, words, &mut bool)`, `popover(icon, Popover)`, `menu(icon, words, closure)`, `custom(name, width, closure)` (fields, combo boxes, segmented controls, notes; `more(width, closure)` gives it a form in More) and `separator`. Modifiers: `in_more` (always in More), `yields`, `unfolds`, `icon_only` (also for a popover's or menu's button: the compact header's icon and chevron), `enabled`, `tip`, `look`, `words_icon`. Header settings: `more_words` ("More ▾" rather than `⋯`), `more_after(name)`, `end_separator`. Everything is measured in the same frame, so a width always gives the same row, both ways. The yields are taken one step at a time: an unfolded popover folds (from the right group's end), the left group's words go from its end to its start, then the right group's, then items move into More in the same order (the items marked `primary`, Save, last of all: corrected in the review below, B3). The end slot keeps the right end at every width. Where even More does not fit beside it, More and what is in it are cut, as is any item that may not yield and reaches More or the end slot. Cut items are not drawn and are recorded as `Place::Cut`. More holds, in the row's order, buttons as rows, toggles as check rows, a popover's rows under a heading (its switch a check row), a menu's rows under a heading, and a custom item's More form. A line at the right group's end stays where More follows it (the Script board's codepage, a line, `⋯`). The row takes no stop of Tab, so Tab goes from item to item; a right click anywhere on it, over its items too, opens `HeaderOutput::context_menu` (corrected in the review below, B1). It records each item's `Place`, and an unfolded popover's switch and rows, in its output and per header (`header::records`, `places` the first; corrected in the review below, B4). One popover of a header is open at a time. Its rectangle is `HeaderOutput::popover_rect`.
- **`popover`: `Popover`, `PopCheck`, `PopAction`, `Hang`, `PopoverOutput`, `paint_switch`.** It is described once and shown three ways: `show(ui, &button)` (open state kept under the button's id), `show_hung(ctx, id, anchor, frame, &mut open)` (the header's), `show_in_place` (a catalog's) and `show_unfolded` (measured first by `unfolded_width`). Its parts are a title (`.pop-title`, 28 px, a line, 4 under it) with an optional master switch (26 by 14), whose rows are said disabled while it is off and keep their values (`rows_follow_switch`). Then sub headings and separators (the kit's `menu_heading` and `menu_separator`), check rows (box, optional icon, words; `sub` 32 px in; lit `hover-row`), action rows (icon, words, a weak note at the right end; chosen, they close the popover and are named in `clicked`), and rows of the caller's (`custom`, or `custom_unfolded` with a form for the row). It is at least 220 wide, `panel` with a `line-soft` border, radius 4 and `--shadow`, 4 px under its button. `Hang::BelowRight` aligns right edges, so the popover never reaches right of its button; `Hang::Auto` resolves by the button's side of the header's middle. **It is the kit's own `Area`, not an egui popup**, because egui keeps one popup open at a time: a combo box's list opened from a row would close it (the NUI designer's known difference). It closes on a click outside it and its button, or on Escape, except while an egui popup is open over it (checked as the frame begins). It is kept inside the window: moved, never flipped to the other side of its button. Since egui's popup memory does not see it, `Popover::any_open(ctx)` says whether one was open this pass or the last. U3 adds it to what Escape's rule (`widgets::dialogs`' `escape`) and `shell::keys_blocked` ask, beside `egui::Popup::is_any_open`, so that an Escape closing a popover (or `header_holds` closing More) cancels no docked editor. Unfolded, a check row is a toggle (its icon, or its words with `unfolded_words`), an action a word button, a separator a line, and the master switch a toggle with the title's icon. A popover with a row lacking an unfolded form does not unfold (`can_unfold`).
- **`shelf`: `ToolShelf`, `Tool`, `ShelfOutput`, `TOOLS`.** A 36 px strip of `bar` with a `line` at its right, padding 4 and gap 2. Tools are 28 px, frameless in `text-2`, `raised` hovered, `accent-fill` on, and `text-off` disabled (not faded). Separators are 20 by 1 of `line-soft`. It is one group named "Tools", each tool said on or not, its tip its name where none is given.
- **`shell::VSEP_WIDTH`** (9) names the width `vsep` already had.

Tests (each seen to fail once with its behavior broken; the breaks and their failures are in the task's report; the review below found two orders the yields test did not hold, G1 and G9, which it holds since). The kit's `tests/kit/header.rs`: `the_header_yields_words_then_icons_then_more` narrows a script-like header in 10 px steps from 1400 to its end slot's width and widens it again. It asserts the end slot's place and width, nothing past the row or over the end slot, monotone forms, the order (the left group's words from its end, nothing into More while a word keeps its words), the same row both ways, and every name found once on the row or in More. Further tests: `more_holds_buttons_and_toggles`, `a_popover_hangs_below_right_never_over_the_top_right_corner`, `a_master_switch_turns_its_rows_off_and_back`, `a_combo_inside_a_popover_leaves_it_open`, `an_action_row_closes_its_popover`, `a_popover_button_can_be_kept_to_its_icon`, `tab_goes_from_item_to_item`, `a_menu_opens_from_its_button_and_a_row_closes_it`, `more_holds_menus_parts_and_popovers` (a menu's rows, a custom item's More form, a popover's switch and rows, More kept inside a narrow window), `more_stands_where_it_is_asked` (`more_after`, `more_words`), `a_line_stands_before_more`, `an_open_popover_is_seen_open`, `the_tool_shelf_reports_its_tools`, `a_disabled_kit_button_is_not_faded` (its text unfaded in `text-off`, its fill `raised`, in both themes) and `auto_hangs_by_the_side_of_the_middle`; and `button.rs`'s two unit tests of the looks' tokens. The kit sheet gains a cell of the header at four widths (unfolded, as given, words gone, things in More), the kit button's states, the Lighting and a Brush popover in place, and a shelf (`target/ui-mock/impl/m4-u1/`, dark and light, with the sheet before).

`common::header_holds(h, group, names)` (in `crates/mg-ui/tests/app/common.rs`, generic over the harness's state) reads `header::records`. It finds each name on the row within the header's group (for an unfolded popover, one of its rows), drawn inside the row and clear of the end slot and More, or opens More and finds it in `More in <group>`, drawn inside its popover, then closes More with Escape (the rectangles: corrected in the review below, A2; `header_holds_nth` for the second header of a name). A name cut or missing fails, named. `yields::a_header_holds_its_controls_down_to_its_least_width` drives a bare kit header with it from 1200 to its least width.

How the editors write their headers with it, unchanged:

- **U3, the area.** The left group holds the kind toggles as one `joined` group (since the review, C3; All and None on an `icon_only` menu beside it; under E 990, by `Yields::header_compact`, a `popover(Eye, "Show")` of check rows and All and None actions, its words carrying the count), Snap and Turn as `custom` combos (their `compact` form without value text, given under 550 by `Yields::value_text`, or taken where the row narrows: since the review, D4), and Together and Onto Objects as icon toggles (`in_more` under 490, by `Yields::into_more`). The right group holds Day | Night as a `custom` segmented control (compact: one moon toggle), then Lighting, Overlays (`switch(&mut on, "Show overlays")`; Walkmesh's menu folds into its rows) and Audio as `popover(…).unfolds()` (compact: `.icon_only()`; the wide row unfolds them where they fit, Fade Geometry and Shadows by `custom_unfolded`), Area Properties as `button` (compact: `icon_only`) and the warning as a `custom` item. To Scratch and Save as Prefab… are `in_more`. `HeaderOutput::context_menu` is `toolbar_menu`, and a hidden tool is an item left out.
- **U4.** Paint's options take the left group (a `custom` segmented control, `custom` number fields, a Brush `popover` whose combo boxes stay open) and the shelf is `ToolShelf`.
- **U12, the script.** Save and Compile are `button`s; Save As…, Print…, Open… and External Editor `icon`s; Find… and Replace… `custom` (joined) or two `icon`s; Bookmark an icon `toggle`; Definition, References, Rename Symbol… and Used By `icon`s; To Scratch is `in_more`. The codepage is a right `custom` item followed by a `separator`, which stays before More.
- **U14, the conversation** (Conversation_crop_header). Add is a `button`; Copy, Cut, Paste, Paste As Link, Delete, the arrows, Expand All and Collapse All are `icon`s between `separator`s. The right group holds the five highlights as a `joined` group (since the review, C3), Scripts as a `toggle` with its icon and words, Export as a `menu` with `words_icon(true)`, Import Lines… as a `button` and Test as a `button`; OK and Cancel go in the end slot. The header keeps the body's 13 points whatever the editor's own text size (Options › Conversation Editor): it is chrome, as the Top Bar is, and the 30-point row could not hold larger text. Since the review (D9) the header sets the kit's sizes back for its row and popovers itself, so U14 draws it where it likes; today's toolbar, which grows with that setting, does not.
- **U16 on.** The 2DA editor's Add Row count, Find, Go to and "N cells are wrong" are `custom`; Values as names and Strings as text are right icon `toggle`s; it uses `more_words(true)` with its four `in_more` items; its own Undo and Redo go. The talk table's Export is a `menu` with `words_icon(true)`, and Close (read-only) the end slot. The NUI designer's pages are a `custom` segmented control, Build & Compile a `button` with `look(Primary)` and `icon_only()`, More stands after Export… (`more_after`), and View is a `popover` with combo rows. The tileset's pages are a `custom` segmented control. The hak's Remove (3) is `words`, its search a `custom` field (the kit's header replaces `Tools::fitted`). The journal, factions, read-only resource and Options are the same items and the end slot. Every editor's narrowing test ends in `header_holds` at its least width.

Not built here, by the plan: the compass, the view's corner buttons and the readout chip (U4, U8), and the page list, footer and gallery cell (later tasks). Nothing in `mg-ui` draws the new parts yet. No deferred entry is settled or added (230 before and after). The next tasks inherit:

- popover rows have no hovered state to force for a catalog, as the menu's do (`as_hovered`);
- a popover's check rows have no keyboard focus ring of their own beyond the row's fill;
- `Popover::any_open` is not yet asked by the application's Escape rule (U3 wires it, with the first popover the application shows).

**Review, 2026-11-15.** An independent review of U1's change (ca15bfe2) found seven faults, settled seven suspicions, three differences from the boards and seven gaps in the API later tasks need. Each fix has a test, seen to fail with its fix taken out; the failure text is quoted. The new tests are in `crates/mg-uikit/tests/kit/header_fixes.rs` unless named otherwise.

Faults:

- **A1. An item's `enabled(false)` was dropped** in More (a popover's, a menu's, a custom item's form), unfolded, and for a custom item on the row: a disabled popover's Grid row flipped in More. The fix carries `enabled` into each of them. `Popover::enabled` disables the switch and every row, hung, unfolded and in More; a menu's rows in More are drawn in a disabled `Ui`; a custom item's `Ui` is disabled on the row, in its compact form and in More; a joined group's buttons are disabled too. Tests, one per place:
  - `a_disabled_popover_is_disabled_in_more`: "assertion failed: … get_by_label("Grid").accesskit_node().is_disabled()";
  - `a_disabled_menu_is_disabled_in_more`: "… get_by_label("CSV…") … is_disabled()";
  - `a_disabled_custom_item_is_disabled_everywhere`: "row enabled: [("row", true)]" with the row's fix out; "the More form enabled: [("more", false), ("more", true), …]" with the More form's;
  - `a_disabled_popover_is_disabled_unfolded`: "an unfolded row of a disabled popover";
  - `a_disabled_joined_group_is_disabled`: "assertion failed: node.accesskit_node().is_disabled()".
- **A2. `header_holds` proved only that a node existed.** The header now records the row's, the end slot's, More's and an open popover's rectangles (`header::records`). `header_holds` checks each control lies inside the row and clear of the end slot and More, or inside More's popover. With the row's items drawn 5000 to the right, `yields::a_header_holds_its_controls_down_to_its_least_width` fails: "Save is drawn outside Kit header's row: [[5014.0 11.5] - [5077.0 33.5]] in [[8.0 8.0] - [1192.0 38.0]]".
- **A3, and B9 with it. More kept its old width after its own button closed it.** The popover's "shown" flag was cleared only by `show_hung`, which is not called on that close. Every close path, a popover giving way to another, and a header that missed a pass now call `Popover::forget` (public, as is `Popover::focus_in`). `more_is_measured_again_after_any_close` failed: "More kept its width: 332 after 332".
- **A4. A header popover reopened by itself** when its header came back after a pass not drawn (another tab in front). The open item is kept with the pass it was drawn in and dropped when the header missed one. `a_popover_does_not_reopen_by_itself` failed: "the popover came back by itself".
- **A5. `controls::box_painter` divided the fade out in an enabled `Ui`** too, lifting a parent's opacity (a fading window's) to full for a `KitButton` disabled by itself. It now divides only where egui faded, in a disabled `Ui`. **This changes existing kit code (the add-only rule):** its other callers (check box, radio, segmented control, a menu's check and radio rows) pass `ui.is_enabled()`, or the response's enabled state, which egui takes from the `Ui`. Where they pass false the `Ui` is disabled, so they look as before. `a_disabled_kit_button_keeps_its_parent_s_opacity` failed: "Save: #5F_66_72_FF" (alpha FF under a parent's 0.5); the extended unit test `controls::tests::a_disabled_box_keeps_a_parent_s_opacity` failed: "1".
- **A6. Tab did not go into an open popover.** A popover opened by the keyboard (a click with no pointer click: Enter or Space) now takes the focus to its first row that takes it (the switch, a check or an action row), and Tab goes on through its rows. This covers the header's popovers and `Popover::show`. `the_keyboard_goes_into_a_popover_it_opened` failed: "the focus stayed outside".
- **A7. Under the end slot's width the end slot was drawn outside the row** (a 60 px window: from −74 to 46). Every part's `Ui` is now clipped to the row. The least width is documented in `header`'s comment: the end slot's and the padding, `end_width + 12`. `the_end_slot_stays_inside_a_row_too_narrow` failed: "drawn outside the row: [[0.0 0.0] - [60.0 100.0]]".

Suspicions, each settled:

- **B1, a fault. A right click on an item never reached the row**, only one on a gap. `HeaderOutput::secondary_clicked` is now true for a right click anywhere on the row, and `HeaderOutput::context_menu(add)` opens the header's own menu at the pointer in the kit's menu style. `HeaderOutput::responses` and `response(name)` give each item's response (D1). `a_right_click_anywhere_on_the_row_reaches_it` failed with the row's own `secondary_clicked()` as the condition: "assertion `left == right` failed: a right click on Save and on a gap".
- **B2, a fault. A menu closed on any click in its rows' rectangle**, headings and disabled rows included. It now closes only where the widget egui reports clicked is an enabled widget inside the rows that senses a click and not a drag (a button). The popover's own `Area` and a selectable label, whose text a drag selects, are reported clicked too and are left out. `a_menu_closes_on_an_enabled_row_only` failed: "closed by a heading or a disabled row".
- **B3, the owner's ruling. Save moves into More last.** `Item::primary()` marks a header's first commands (one or more); they move into More after everything else that may, the right group's items included. Otherwise the left group goes from its end, as before. Their words go at the left group's step, as others' do. The order is tested in two places:
  - `the_first_command_goes_into_more_last`: "Save into More before Day at 350";
  - the yields test, now with Save marked: "Save into More before Day at 370".

  Guard G1, More filled from the left group's start, now fails the yields test: "Compile into More before Open… at 550" (all sixteen header tests had passed with it).
- **B4, a fault. Two headers of one name shared one record.** Records are kept per header id, `header::records(ctx, name)` gives each header's in the order their rows stand (`HeaderRecord`), and `places` gives the first's. `common::header_holds_nth` takes which header; `yields::two_headers_of_one_name_are_each_held` drives two. `two_headers_of_one_name_keep_a_record_each` failed with one record per name: "assertion `left == right` failed".
- **B5, a fault. A tall popover had no scroll** and climbed over its button in a short window. A hung popover is now no taller than the room under its button (or over it, hung above); what does not fit scrolls. `a_tall_popover_scrolls_under_its_button` failed: "over its button: [[14.0 0.0] - [244.0 260.0]]".
- **B6, a fault. The shelf clipped its tools silently.** Where it is too short, the tools that do not fit go into a **More Tools** tool at its foot (`more-vertical`, on while a tool in it is on), whose popover, hung above it (`Hang::Above`, D7), lists them; one chosen there is reported clicked by its index, as on the shelf. `a_short_shelf_puts_its_last_tools_in_more` failed: "No nodes found matching the query" (no More Tools).
- **B7. The master switch's role.** accesskit 0.24 has `Role::Switch`, which egui's widget types do not offer. The switch now replaces its node's role, keeping its name and toggled state. `the_master_switch_is_a_switch` failed: "assertion `left == right` failed" (`CheckBox`). A disabled popover's switch is drawn in the disabled tokens.
- **B8, a fault. An action row drawn in place called `ui.close()`**, which closed a closable parent of the catalog's. `show_in_place` now gives the body a closable scope of its own. `an_action_in_place_closes_no_parent` failed: "the catalog's parent was asked to close".
- **B9** is A3's.

The boards:

- **C1.** A word button's icon alone (the left group's words gone), and a popover's or menu's compact icon and chevron, are now framed (`raised`, the icon in `text`), as Narrow.png draws Lighting, Overlays, Audio and Area Properties. `ButtonLook::Flat` stays `.btn-icon` (`Item::icon`, More's `⋯`). `button::tests` failed with the old rule: "assertion `left == right` failed".
- **C2.** A disabled primary is a disabled word button, `raised` and `text-off`, because `.btn:disabled` wins over `.btn.primary`. `a_disabled_button_takes_the_disabled_tokens` failed: "assertion `left == right` failed".
- **C3.** `Item::joined(name, Vec<Joined>)`: a group of icon toggles and buttons (`Joined::toggle`, `Joined::button`) 1 px apart, its outer corners round (`KitButton::joined(first, last)`). It yields as one, straight into More, where it is a heading of its name over a check row per toggle and a row per button. Its buttons are recorded in its place. `a_joined_group_yields_as_one` failed with a 4 px gap: "a 1 px seam: [[14.0 11.5] - [36.0 33.5]] [[40.0 11.5] - [62.0 33.5]]".
- **C4.** The pressed or focused ring (`accent`, 1 px) is kept, consistent with `paint_icon_button`.

API added:

- **D1:** `HeaderOutput::responses` and `response(name)`.
- **D3:** `Popover::custom_unfolded(name, …)`: the row is named, so `check_names` and the header's record name it where it unfolds. `an_unfolded_custom_row_is_recorded` failed: "assertion failed: places.borrow().contains(&("Fade Geometry"…))".
- **D4:** `Item::compact(width, add)`: a custom item's compact form, taken at the step where word buttons lose their words. This is how U3's Snap and Turn yield. `a_custom_item_takes_its_compact_form` failed: "assertion `left == right` failed" (Row, Row, More where Row, Icon, More).
- **D5:** `EditorHeader::set_open(Some(name) | None)` opens or closes a header popover from code; `HeaderOutput::open` says which is open. `a_header_popover_opens_from_code` failed: "assertion `left == right` failed".
- **D7:** `Hang::Above`. `a_popover_can_hang_above` failed: "[[8.0 334.0] - [230.0 368.0]] [[8.0 308.0] - [77.0 330.0]]".
- **D9:** the header sets back the kit's text sizes (`theme::kit_sizes`: the body's 13 points, a control's 22) for its row, its measurements and its popovers, whatever the window's style. `a_larger_text_does_not_grow_the_header` failed with the row's style left as given: "…(30.0, 63.0, 20.0)…" (a custom item's text at 20).

Documents:

- **E1:** `Place::Row` and `Place::Icon` say what each form is for each kind.
- **E2:** `Item::words_icon` changes popovers' and menus' buttons only.
- **E3:** `Item::enabled` lists where it applies. The Done paragraph's claims are corrected in place, each marked.
- **E4:** the yields test's comment. Guard G9, an unfolded popover never folding first, now fails it: "assertion `left != right` failed: words went before the fold at 870".
- **E5:** the agdb note says `header_holds` checks rectangles, and why.

The kit sheet shows the joined group, the framed icon forms and a disabled primary, Dark and Light, in `target/ui-mock/impl/m4-u1-fix/`, against Narrow.png and Foundations.

New `pub`:

- `Joined`, `Item::joined`, `Item::compact`, `Item::primary`;
- `EditorHeader::set_open`;
- `HeaderOutput::secondary_clicked`, `responses`, `response`, `open`, `context_menu`;
- `HeaderRecord`, `records`, `JOIN_SEAM`;
- `KitButton::joined`;
- `Popover::enabled`, `forget`, `focus_in`, `Hang::Above`;
- `shelf::MORE_TOOLS`.

Changed signatures: `Popover::custom_unfolded` takes a name first; `header::places` reads the first record.

No deferred entry is settled or added (230).

**U2. The docks hold what an editor brings (2 days).**

- **Builds.** Part 2, decision 2: `Dock::Bottom`, sections, `editor_brings`, panel bodies with two hosts, the late slot, per-kind layouts in `Settings`, N Bottom Dock tabs and N Status Bar switches with badges. The area's Palette and Preview move onto it unchanged.
- **User sees.** Nothing (the Right Dock as today; the Log alone below).
- **Tests.** Unit: sections folded into one bar under 1400 and split into columns in the wide layout; the switches' widths for one to five switches, as `status_bar.rs`'s sweep does for one. App: every `right_dock.rs` and `status_bar.rs` test unchanged; a dock with no available panel is not drawn.
- **Timing.** `frame_perf` (the docks are drawn every frame).
- **Hands on.** Sections, `editor_brings` and the Bottom Dock's tabs to U5 and U6 (the area), U11 to U13 (blueprints, script) and every editor after.
- **Coherent.** Yes.
