**Done, 2026-11-15.** **Built** (all in `crates/mg-ui/src/shell.rs` unless named). `Panel` gains `Log`, the Bottom Dock's global panel, and `Dock` gains `Bottom`. What an editor brings is `Brings` (the Outliner; `right`, the Right Dock's sections from the top, each a static slice of panels; `folded`, their order in one bar under 1400; `bottom`, the editor's own Bottom Dock panels; `floats`, whether they follow the editor into its window), given by `brings_of(app, tab)` by the tab's kind and `editor_brings(app)` for the active docked editor; it replaces `editor_brings_palette` and `editor_brings_outliner`, and `Panel::available` reads it (`available_with` for a floating editor's). The area brings the Outliner and one section, Palette and Preview (with the game's data and a module), the placeholder the section alone. `right_sections`, `panels` (the Right Dock's in the folded order, the Bottom Dock's own then `Panel::BOTTOM_TABS`), `switches` (`SWITCHES_BEFORE`, the editor's own, `SWITCHES_AFTER`) and `arrange` (sections, folded order, narrow, columns in; columns of stacked tab bars out) are plain functions. `right_dock` draws one bar exactly as before (same ids, names, nesting), or stacked sections each with its own bar over its share (`section_rects`, `section_heights`; the seams, `section_seams`, made after the sections' widgets so that they are over them, keep the shares per kind when dragged), or the wide layout's two columns (first section, then the rest; a lone section of two stands as its first panel and the rest, today's Palette | Preview, so the user sees nothing until U6 adds a second section). A section's front is the dock's panel chosen last where it holds it, else the run's last choice among its panels (`Moonglow::right_fronts`), else the settings' for the kind, else its first (`section_front`, `choose_right`); fronts and shares are kept by the section's place among the kind's own sections (`own_index`), never for a section it does not bring (the palettes asked for by name beside a script write nothing for the script). `panel_body(app, ui, panel, Host, pane)` is a panel's one body, its host the dock or the editor's window (`window_panes`, called from `TabViewer::ui` for a tab in a window whose kind `floats`: the sections stacked in a right pane by the kind's shares, with the same seams, the own Bottom Dock panels on a bottom pane's tabs, before the editor draws in what is left). The late slot is `LateSlot` (panel, rect, layer, pane) in `Moonglow::late_slots`, drawn by `draw_late` after the editor area (the Preview's by `area_tools::preview_late`, the old `preview_panel`). `bottom_dock` has a tab per panel it holds, the panel in front's tools (`Panel::header_tools`: the Log's Copy, Save… and Clear; the tools' width the widest of the panels', so the tabs keep their form), the front from `bottom_shown` (chosen in the run, else the kind's kept, else the first: the Log today); `status_bar` has a `StatusSwitch` per panel with its `Panel::badge(app)`, on while the dock shows that panel, a click on an off switch opening the dock on it and on an on switch closing it; the icons-alone arithmetic is `switches_width` (each switch's width and `status::GAP` between them), the Log's one switch giving the old sum. `Settings::dock_layouts` (kind name to `DockLayout { fronts, shares, bottom }`, by panel names) is written only once something is chosen (`skip_serializing_if`, so files stay as they were), read where kinds or panels are a newer version's (passed by), and cleared by Reset Layout; an older Moonglow ignores the field (serde's default), tested. F6 (`step_pane`), the keys leaving undrawn panels and `show_dock_panel` go by the bars drawn (`Moonglow::right_bars`) and the Bottom Dock's front instead of the Palette and Preview by name; `palettes_asked` is set only for those two. **Decided**: (1) **The area's front tab was not kept in the settings** (superseded: the owner ruled on 2026-11-15 that the area remembers its tab too; see the Review paragraph): decision 2 keeps fronts per kind in `Settings`, but M3's `the_palette_is_in_front_as_a_module_opens` kept the Right Dock's tab for the run and put the Palette in front as a module opened, so `keeps_fronts` exempted the area and the placeholder. (2) **Orders are data**: the folded order, the Bottom Dock's tab order and the switches' order each come from tables (the Script board's tabs are Compiler, Bookmarks, Search Results, Log and its switches Log, Problems, Compiler, Bookmarks, Search Results, which no single order gives). (3) **The plugins' pane** (decision 2's last but one point, written into 17-plugin-api.md): a press in a panel an editor brings makes that editor's pane the pane last clicked in: the Palette and the Preview the palette, as before; an area's other panels the area; an editor with no pane of its own leaves it as it was. (4) **Test panels**: the generalization is proved in the library's own tests (`shell::dock_tests`, kittest in `src` as `options.rs` has it) with `Panel::Sample(n)` and `Moonglow::sample_brings`, both `#[cfg(test)]`: built into neither the application nor `tests/app` nor `screens.rs`, so nothing fake ships. **Pictures**: `shell_main`, `shell_wide`, every `shell_width_*` (1600 among them), `shell_760`, `board_main`, `board_narrow`, `board_wide`, `board_floating`, Dark and Light, before (the tip `52d640be`) and after: all 44 the same to the pixel (`target/ui-mock/impl/m4-u2/`). **Timing**: `frame_perf` release, each run alone, at 1-minute loads of 2.7 to 3.0 at their starts; the machine shared, so the tip (`52d640be`, built apart from a `git archive` copy) and U2's tree were run interleaved, tip, U2, U2, tip (`target/ui-mock/perf-m4/u2/`, `agree.txt`): of 339 views the median difference is +0.015 ms in each U2 run (median view 1.91 against 1.93 and 2.00 ms; tessellation 55.8 against 58.0 and 57.4 ms summed); 28 views moved over 0.3 ms the same way in both U2 runs, both ways and mostly 3D views (the grass field, area026, lake) that no dock change touches, where the two tip runs alone differ by over 0.3 ms in 72 views (`noise_tip1_tip2.txt`): no view moved beyond the noise. Over the 6.94 ms budget in both U2 runs only the views over it in the tip runs too (area084 under the wheel and with all selected, as in U0's logs). One U2 run failed `reload_frames_at_144` on a single 11.3 ms frame of its second reload pass (median 0.71 ms, as the tip's); the other U2 run and both tip runs passed it (longest 3.4 to 3.9 ms); run alone three times more on U2's final tree (loads 6.0 to 6.8), it passed each time (longest 3.7 to 4.1 ms, `reload_1.log` to `reload_3.log`). **Not built, by the plan**: no panel beyond the area's (none has a feeder yet: decision 2's rule), so no shipped kind has two sections, a floating panel or its own Bottom Dock panel; the wide layout's column seam stays a line (U6); a window's panes' sizes are egui's for the run, not kept. **How the later tasks plug in** (with what the review added: the editor passed to bodies, badges and the late slot, the Preview brought alone, the globals that follow into a window, `owner_of`): **U5** adds `Panel::TileProperties` (dock Right, its body an arm of `panel_body`, available while tiles are chosen) and has `brings_of` give the area a second section, `[[Palette, Preview], [TileProperties]]`, while Select Tiles is on (a second static table chosen by the area's state). **U6** adds `Panel::Properties` and makes the area's table `[[Palette, Preview], [Properties]]` with `folded: [Palette, Properties, Preview]`: `arrange` then gives Main's two stacked sections, Narrow's three tabs and WideArea's Palette and Preview in the first column and Properties in the second (the lone-section rule no longer applies); Q1's double click is `show_dock_panel(Panel::Properties)`. **U12** adds `Reference` and `Help`, a `Tab::Script` arm `Brings { right: [[Reference], [Help]], floats: true, .. }`; docked they fill the Right Dock, floating `window_panes` draws them in the script's window (Floating.png), the script's own side pane retired. **U13** adds `Compiler` and `Bookmarks` to the script's `bottom` (their switches come after the Log's, before the global ones after), and **Search Results** as a global panel: `BOTTOM_TABS = [SearchResults, Log]`, `SWITCHES_AFTER = [SearchResults]`, its hits its `badge`, its availability its feeder's. **U14** gives `Tab::Dialog` `right: [[Node, Data, Search], [Conversation]]`, `floats: true`. **U20** adds **Problems**: `SWITCHES_BEFORE = [Log, Problems]`, a tab in `BOTTOM_TABS`, its errors its badge (`BadgeKind::Error`). A new panel needs its `Panel` arms (`dock`, `name`, `icon`, `tools`, `header_tools`, `strip_tip`, `command`, `keys`, `badge`, `available_with`, `named`'s list) and a body arm in `panel_body` (and in `bottom_dock`'s match only if it draws itself, as the log does); a panel that takes the arrow keys with a `Keys` of its own is named in `Panel::KEYED` (since the review; `keys_leave_panels_not_drawn` goes by what each dock draws).

**Review, 2026-11-15.** An independent review of U2 (its commit `546462ac`) found two faults shown, four suspected and seven gaps later tasks would have had to change U2's code for; the owner ruled on one. Each is fixed below, each new or changed test seen to fail with its fix taken out of the source (the failure text in brackets; the breaks in `target/u2/breaks/run2.py`). **The owner's ruling (2026-11-15): "The area should remember its tab too."** `keeps_fronts` is gone: every kind keeps its section fronts in `Settings::dock_layouts`, the area's too, under the kind `layout_kind` gives (the placeholder that stands for no area keeps the area's). The fault the reviewer found under it is fixed: `section_front` returned `Moonglow::right_panel` whenever the section held it, and that was set to the Palette at start and as a module opened, so a kept front was never read; `right_panel` is now `Option<Panel>`, `None` at start and again as a module opens (with the run's choices), so the kept front is shown. `preview_panel::the_palette_is_in_front_as_a_module_opens` is now `the_area_remembers_the_right_dock_s_tab`: with nothing chosen the Palette, the Preview's tab clicked, the Preview after reopening the module and after a restart (the settings saved and read back) [with the module's reset to the Palette put back: "after reopening: left: Some(Palette), right: Some(Preview)"; the start value alone is not a separate case: every run opens its module through the same path]. The manual says so (02, the docks). **Shown.** (A1) **Two windows' settings**: `Settings::saved_over` merged only the fields this window's text had, and `dock_layouts`, written only where not empty, is missing from a text with none, so an untouched window dropped the other's layouts and undid its Reset Layout. It now merges over every field either text (or the one this window read) has, a missing field a value like any other; files stay byte-identical (the trade: none, the field still left out when empty). `a_window_saves_its_own_changes_over_another_window_s` gains the three cases [the old merge: "theirs taken: left: {}, right: {\"script\": …}"]. (A2) settled by the ruling. **Suspected, each a fault.** (B1) A window's panes were carved bottom first, the bottom pane the window's whole width; the right pane is now carved first, the whole height, the bottom pane under the editor alone (Floating.png); `a_floating_editor_s_panels_are_in_its_window` checks the two panes' places and `a_seam_dragged_keeps_the_sections_shares` measures the right pane's share against its own height [the panes carved bottom first: "[301 520]-[1199 699] beside [920 129]-[1199 519]"]. (B2) `Brings::window_bottom` names the global Bottom Dock panels that follow the editor into its window, after its own (U13: the floating script's Search Results); `a_window_s_bottom_pane_holds_the_globals_its_kind_names` ["left: [Sample 8, Sample 9], right: [Sample 8, Sample 9, Log]"]. (B3) A bare press in a stacked section and F6 wrote `fronts`: they now only move the dock's panel (`focus_right`); a tab clicked, a strip button or a panel asked for by name chooses (`choose_right`), kept; `only_a_tab_chosen_is_kept` [the press choosing: "{\"welcome\": … fronts: [\"\", \"Sample 1\"]}"; F6 choosing: "fronts: [\"Sample 0\"]"]. (B4) `keys_leave_panels_not_drawn` goes by what each dock draws, the Bottom Dock among them, with no panel named in its arms: `Panel::KEYED` lists the panels with arrow keys of their own, each dock's front is the hand-over; the log's keys leave a closed Bottom Dock for the editor, or go to the tab in front; `the_keys_leave_the_log_where_it_is_not_drawn` [the Bottom Dock taken as drawn: "the dock closed"; no hand-over: "to the tab in front"]. **Gaps.** (C1) `panel_body`, `LateSlot` (`editor`, with its `host`) and `Panel::badge` take the editor they draw or count for: the active docked editor in a dock, the floating editor in its window; the floating test checks each body and late body is drawn for `Tab::Manual`, not the docked Welcome page [the editor not passed: "(Sample(0), Window, None)…"]. (C2) A kind may bring the Preview alone (`available_with`: the kind's own where it brings no Palette, else with the palettes); the palettes asked for by name beside it add the Palette alone, no second Preview; a Preview body in a window is salted with its pane (`LateSlot::salted`; the dock's keeps its old `"preview-panel"`), so the dock's and a window's draw in one frame; `a_kind_brings_the_preview_alone` [a duplicate: "left: [Palette, Preview], right: [Palette]"; the Preview bound to the Palette: no Right Dock at all]. (C3) View › Log shows the Log in front of the Bottom Dock and is ticked only while it is (`panel_shown`, `show_panel`); Hide All Panels and the switches open and close the dock as a whole; each tool of the panel in front is enabled as its own command is (`Id::enabled`), not by the log's emptiness; `view_log_shows_the_log_tab` [the old tick: "another tab in front: not ticked"]. (C4) Plugin panels: not needed, by ruling Q3 (they stay tabs or windows). (C5) A window's panes are stops of F6 after the editor area (`Moonglow::window_bars`, the last frame's) and a press in one makes its editor's pane the plugins' pane (`plugin_pane` takes the editor); `f6_stops_at_a_window_s_panes` ["the window's pane reached"], `a_brought_panel_is_its_editor_s_pane_for_plugins` (a press in a floating area's window pane) ["left: None, right: Some(Area(area001))"]. (C6) `owner_of(app, tab)` resolves a tab to the editor it belongs to, whose panels it brings and whose layout it keeps: every tab is its own today, U19 maps the NUI designer's source group to the designer so focusing the source keeps its Right Dock; scripts unchanged (the ruling at T9's review); `the_placeholder_s_layout_is_the_area_s` [the placeholder's own kind: "left: \"no-area\", right: \"area\""]. (C7) The generalized paths (stacked sections, seams, floating panes, Bottom Dock tabs, the switches for several panels, a dock with no available panel) stay covered by the library's tests: they drive the whole `Moonglow::ui` through kittest as the app tests do, and only there can a `#[cfg(test)]` panel exist; no shipped panel yet has those shapes (the area's one section and the Log are covered at the app tier by every `right_dock.rs`, `status_bar.rs`, `preview_panel.rs` and `yields.rs` test). **Docs.** (D1) the Done paragraph's "with no change to what U2 built", its keys sentence and its decision (1) say what the code now does; (D2) the old-reader case of `the_docks_layouts_per_kind_read_and_write_both_ways` reads the newer text with a stand-in struct (`Older`, two fields), as the precedent test does, not with an older build; (D3) 17-plugin-api.md: an area's other panels count as the area's view, in the docks or the editor's window; (D4) `Panel::tools` counts a panel's own list (`header_tools`) instead of a second number; `right_shown` no longer joins the sections into a new list each call (`right_sections` still makes its few small lists: cheap beside a frame, `frame_perf` showed no cost). **Pictures**: the same eight cases, Dark and Light, into `target/ui-mock/impl/m4-u2-fix/`: all 44 the same to the pixel as before U2 (the tip `52d640be`): the ruling shows only once a tab is chosen (the pictures' settings choose none, so the Palette is in front as before), and B1 places only a floating editor's panes, which no shipped kind brings yet. The whole suite and the gates were run after the last edit.

**U3. The area header and its popovers (2 days).**

- **Builds.** The Main, Narrow and WideArea headers: the kind toggles as icons with All and None on the group's arrow; Snap and Turn combos; Together and Onto Objects; Day | Night; the **Lighting** popover (Lighting, Shadows with its kinds and quality, Fog, Fade Geometry, Shaders, Grass, Tile Border, Animations), **Overlays** with its master switch (Grid, Minimap, Ground walkmesh, Object walkmeshes, Sound Ranges; today's Walkmesh… menu folds into it), **Audio** (Sounds, Ambient, Music); Area Properties at the right end; More (To Scratch, Save as Prefab…); the warnings icon (Part 2, 8); the header's right-click menu (`toolbar_menu`, `hidden_area_tools`). Yields from `mg_uikit::shell::Yields`: compact under E 990 (Show "5 of 10", the moon, Lighting, Overlays, Audio and Area Properties as icon buttons), Snap and Turn without their values under 550, Together and Onto Objects into More under 490; the wide row unfolded (section 5.3) where it fits. The area stops reading `toolbar_icons_only`; the Options control's tip says it no longer applies to the area.
- **Ticks.** Area Editor: "Show Creatures, Doors…", "All, None", "Snap, Turn", "Together, Onto Objects", "Night", "Lighting, Shadows…", "Shaders, Grass, Tile Border, Animations", "Grid, Minimap…", "Sounds, Ambient, Music", "Area Properties", "To Scratch, Save as Prefab…", "The strip's right-click menu"; At 3440 by 1440, the first bullet; Window at 1280 by 800: "The ten kind toggles and All, None", "Day | Night", "Lighting, Overlays, Audio, Area Properties", and Snap, Turn and More of "Menus, file buttons, Snap, Turn, More, shelf, compass"; How the Shell Yields, its Header line.
- **Settles.** "With Icons and text, the area toolbar takes one row more"; "An open combo box fills `bar`" (the kit's combo, every combo).
- **Tests.** `common::area_tool` finds a control in the "Area header" group or opens the popover that holds it (about 110 lines follow without edits where the helper is used, the rest are edited); `the_toolbar_shows_icons_only_when_asked` becomes "the area header ignores the setting"; new: `header_holds` from 420 to 1600 points; the yields at 989 and 990, 549 and 550, 489 and 490; the Overlays switch off hides its five and on gives them back as they were; a hidden tool gone from row and popover; the wide row's words; read-only disables what edits. Docked and floating (the area is docked in both).
- **Pictures.** `board_main`, `board_narrow`, `board_wide` against their boards and LightArea; `area_toolbar_narrow` renamed `area_header_narrow`.
- **Timing.** `frame_perf` area views (the header's icons and popovers), budget 6.94 ms.
- **Docs.** 04-areas (the toolbar's sections become the header's), 02-main-window (the area header), 10-options (Area › Toolbar names the header); parity "Area viewer". Deferred: keeps "The camera's height" (1795), "Lighting off against Aurora" (2163), "The shadows in two qualities" (2153), "The grid seen from the area's edge" (1689).
- **Hands on.** The header's left group to U4's tools.

**Done, 2026-11-15.** **Built.** The area's toolbar (`area_view::toolbar`, a `horizontal_wrapped` named "Area toolbar") is gone; `area_view/header.rs` (`header`) draws the area's one header row on the kit's `EditorHeader`, named **"Area header"**, by the yields of its own row's width (`mg_uikit::shell::Yields::of(window, ui.available_width())`: E is the row, so a floating window and a split group yield as they are). Left group: the ten Show toggles as a joined group (`Item::joined("Show", …)`) and the board's 16 px arrow of **All** and **None** (a custom item, "Show All or None", a menu; in More its two rows); under 990 one **Show** popover (check rows named "Show Creatures"…, its words "Show N of 10": `Item::shown_words`) with All and None as its action rows. **Snap** and **Turn** are custom combo boxes with their lead icons, as wide as their value (measured in the frame: a value always gives the same row), their compact form the icon and an empty box (taken at the words' step, and under 550 whatever the room), a form for More; named "Snap" and "Turn" on their nodes. **Together** and **Onto Objects** icon toggles, `in_more` under 490. Right group: **Day | Night** a custom segmented control (under 990 one moon toggle, "Night"); the **Lighting** popover (its switch the area's lighting, named "Use the area's lighting", showing "Lighting" unfolded; Shadows' heading, three kinds and Quality as Fast | High, shown hung, and one **Shadows** toggle held or right-clicked for the kinds, shown unfolded; Fog; Fade Geometry a combo; Animations, Grass, Tile border, Shaders), **Overlays** (switch "Show overlays": `AreaView::hide_overlays`, read through `AreaView::overlay` where the grid, the minimap, the walkmeshes and the sound ranges are drawn, each kept as ticked; Grid, Minimap; Ground walkmesh and Object walkmeshes hung, one **Walkmesh** toggle unfolded; Sound ranges), **Audio** (Sounds named "Play Sounds", Ambient, Music), each `unfolds()` (compact: `icon_only()`); **Area Properties** without its icon (compact: its icon, framed); the **warning** (decision 8: a custom item "Warnings", the sign and the count in `warn`, its tip what could not be read, the area's problems and the models not found; a line in More). More holds **To Scratch**, **Save N as Prefab…** and, until U4's shelf and compass, **Select Tiles**, **Paint** (disabled read-only), **Reorient Camera** and **Go to Start Location**. A right click anywhere on the row is `toolbar_menu` ("Show in the Header"), but on the wide row's Shadows and Walkmesh, whose own menus it opens. `hidden_area_tools` keeps its 29 names: a name leaves its item, row or unfolded part out ("Walkmesh…" both walkmeshes and the toggle; "Lighting" the switch), and a popover with nothing left is left out. The area reads no `toolbar_icons_only`; Options' "Editors' toolbar buttons" tip says the header no longer follows it, and Options › Area › Toolbar is **Header**. `icons::Tools` lost what only the area used (`hidden`, `of_area`, `hides`, `showing_all`, `button`, `toggle`, `toggle_as`, `add`). Paint's second strip still follows the header while Paint is on (U4's). **Escape:** `widgets::dialogs_begin`'s escape, `shell::keys_blocked`, `tabs.rs`'s `foresee_escape`, the conversation editor's Escape, `area_view::escaped` and the view's held drag ask `mg_uikit::popover::Popover::any_open` beside egui's popups, so an Escape that closes a header popover (or More) closes nothing else; and `area_view::escaped` asks `widgets::escape_closes_a_popup` (a menu, a list or a kit popover open as the frame began), since a menu has closed by the time the view reads its Escape: with the pointer over the view, which the one-row header made taller, a menu's Escape had also let the selection go. **The kit (add-only, but two changes said here):** `PopCheck::name` (a row named apart from its words), `Popover::switch_words`, `Popover::folded_only` and `Popover::unfolded_only` (rows that stand as one control on the row), `Item::shown_words`, `controls::combo_style` (the open combo box `raised-hover` with its ring: widgets.rs gives it to every combo box of the application, `combo_looks`, only while a popup is open, since a `Ui`'s visuals changed copy its style; the theme's `widgets.open` keeping `bar` for the windows' title bars); changed: `Item::words_icon` now applies to buttons too (no caller had relied on its not), and **two faults of U1's header fixed**: the row's scope left the parent's cursor after its centered items, 8 px into the row, so what followed the header (the area's view) began over its lower half (`advance_cursor_after_rect` after the row); and a popover folded early (from the right group's end) stayed folded where it fit again beside those folded after it (where only popovers were folded, each tries to unfold once more, from the left: the wide board's Audio beside Overlays folded). **Settled:** the known differences "With Icons and text, the area toolbar takes one row more" and "An open combo box fills `bar`". **Decided:** (1) Select Tiles, Paint, Reorient Camera and Go to Start Location stand in More until U4 (the boards draw them on the shelf and under the compass, not on the header; the Main board's row fits only so); (2) popover rows in sentence case as the boards have them, accessible names their words (renames below); the Overlays rows keep their icons (they unfold into icon toggles; the board's popover draws none); (3) the Overlays switch shows in the wide row as an icon toggle (the board leaves it out, but rows grayed with no switch in sight could not be turned back on); (4) Snap's, Turn's and Fade Geometry's boxes are as wide as their value, not their widest, as the Main board draws them. **Differences from the boards left:** Day | Night has no sun and moon icons (the kit's segmented control has words alone); the All or None arrow stands 4 px from the Show toggles, not on a 1 px seam (a joined group holds toggles and buttons, not a menu); the wide row reads "Sound ranges" where WideArea has "Sound Ranges" (a row's words are its unfolded toggle's); the Overlays rows keep their icons; a right click on the wide row's Shadows or Walkmesh opens their own menu, not the header's (the manual says so). **Renamed (accessible names):** the group "Area toolbar" is "Area header"; "Lighting" (the toggle) is the switch "Use the area's lighting" ("Lighting" is now the popover's button); "Shadows" is three check rows, "Sun and moon shadows", "Creature shadows", "Light shadows" (in the wide row the "Shadows" toggle again), the menu's "Light Shadows" etc. likewise; "Walkmesh…" is "Ground walkmesh" and "Object walkmeshes" (in the wide row the "Walkmesh" toggle); "Tile Border" is "Tile border", "Sound Ranges" "Sound ranges"; the right-click menu's heading "Show on the Toolbar" is "Show in the Header". Kept: "Show Creatures"…, "Show Start", "All", "None", "Snap", "Turn", "Together", "Onto Objects", "Night", "Fog", "Fade Geometry", "Animations", "Grass", "Shaders", "Grid", "Minimap", "Play Sounds", "Ambient", "Music", "Select Tiles", "Paint", "Area Properties", "Reorient Camera", "Go to Start Location", "To Scratch", "Save N as Prefab…". **Tests.** `common::header_holds` scrolls a control in a long More into sight before it checks where it is drawn (with everything in More, at the window's least with the Left Dock unfolded, More scrolls). `common::area_tool(h: &mut, name)` finds a control on the row or opens what holds it (by the header's record: a popover, More, the arrow's menu, a held menu) and returns its node, `area_click` clicks it and closes what it opened (`close_area_popover`), `area_tool_shown` reads without opening, `area_header_row` lists the row's nodes; the 42 lines that clicked a toolbar button now call `area_click`, the rest of the 110 follow `area_tool` with `&mut h`; `in_toolbar` knows the header and its popovers. Rewritten: the shadow tests (the popover's rows; the held Shadows toggle in the wide row, `the_shadows_toggle_held_opens_the_kinds_to_choose`; `the_shadows_quality_is_chosen_in_the_lighting_popover`), `the_area_header_s_controls_can_be_hidden` (row, popover and wide row, and the right-click menu anywhere on the row), the walkmesh test (the popover's rows, the wide row's held Walkmesh), `the_area_header_ignores_the_toolbar_buttons_setting` (it was "shows icons only when asked") and the Top Bar's test's area part; the toolbar sweep is replaced by `area_header.rs`: `the_area_header_holds_every_control_from_420_to_1600` (window widths in steps of 20 and the boundaries, both modes, `header_holds` for every item, and every popover row found at four widths), `the_area_header_yields_at_990_550_and_490` (each side of each, the last two with the others hidden), `the_overlays_switch_hides_its_five_and_gives_them_back`, `the_wide_row_unfolds_the_popovers_with_their_words`, `an_escape_that_closes_a_header_popover_closes_nothing_else` (a selection in the view, after a popover's and a menu's Escape, and Area Properties' window), `a_header_popover_in_front_hides_the_view_s_hints` (`shell::keys_blocked`), `the_warning_says_what_could_not_be_read` (on the row with its tip, held in More where the editor is narrowest). The kit's `header_area.rs`: eight tests of the additions and the two fixes (`no_popover_unfolds_again_once_words_went` holds the re-unfolding to rows where only popovers were folded). Each new test was seen to fail with its behavior broken (the task's report lists the breaks). Migrated with the header: `fields::arrow_keys_step_through_the_area_s_lists` (the lists by name, Fade Geometry's in its popover), the palette tests' More (`common::palette_more`, the palette's, not the header's), `prefabs_are_offered_on_the_toolbar_and_in_the_palette` (Save 2 as Prefab… in More), `tree::resources_open_in_their_editors` (the Show popover's button); `status_hints::nothing_in_front_or_no_area_hints` moves the pointer off the view, where a menu's row clicked over the taller view had left it (an Escape closing a dialog with the pointer over the view also lets a selection go, as before U3: not this task's). Two tests' set-ups moved with the view's height (the header is one 30 point row where the toolbar took two to four): `the_sky_under_a_camera_higher_than_the_game_s` keeps the view the size its game picture was held against (the window 782 high), and `hidden_locked_and_hidden_behind_objects_take_no_press` looks from 130°, not straight down the tiles' diagonal, along which their ground's faces are split (a ray down it met the raised tile's corner, or passed by it, as the view's height made it). **Pictures:** `board_main`, `board_narrow`, `board_wide`, the new `area_header_narrow` (the full, compact, 600, 520 and wide rows, Snap's list open, and the Lighting, Overlays, Audio, More, Show and compact Lighting popovers open), `area_shadows_menu` (the Lighting popover's shadows, Light shadows grayed, the wide row's Shadows menu) and `toolbar_icons_only`, Dark and Light, in `target/ui-mock/impl/m4-u3/` with the before pictures. **Timing:** `frame_perf` release, each run alone at 1-minute loads of 2.6 to 3.0 at its start, the tip (`f73d5c38`, a `git archive` copy built apart) and U3's tree interleaved, tip, U3, U3, tip (`target/ui-mock/perf-m4/u3/`, `agree.txt`): of 339 views the median difference is +0.000 and +0.010 ms (median view 1.645 against 1.650 and 1.670 ms), tessellation 40.1 against 38.5 and 39.3 ms summed and some 2,500 vertices fewer in an area's view (one header row, not two to four); three area084 views under the wheel rose 0.4 to 0.6 ms in both U3 runs (the overview, 8.01 to 8.5; the Right Dock in one column, twice), where the two tip runs alone differ by as much in five other area084 views (`noise_tip1_tip2.txt`), and the view is taller under the one row (more of the area drawn each frame). Over the 6.94 ms budget only the four area084 wheel views over it in the tip runs too. **Not built, by the plan:** the shelf, the compass and Paint's options in the left group (U4); the Overlays popover's rows without icons. **The next tasks inherit:** Select Tiles, Paint, Reorient Camera and Go to Start Location to take out of More (U4: the header's `in_more` items; `common::area_tool` finds them wherever they are); `header::records` and `area_tool` for every area test; an accessibility click reaches no widget under a window with Cancel, so a test clicks the header by the pointer there.

**Review, 2026-11-15.** An independent review of U3 (`bdcf5458`) found no control lost and these to fix, each settled with its test, seen to fail with its fix taken out (the break and the failure text in brackets; the breaks in `target/u3/breaks/review.py`).

- **A1. A popover is not modal** (the coordinator's decision). `shell::keys_blocked` no longer counts a kit popover: the view's keys act while one is open (Delete, F10), so the Status Bar keeps their hints; the comment of the area's arm of `shell::shown` and the agdb note say so. Only Escape is the popover's. `a_header_popover_in_front_keeps_the_view_s_hints_and_keys` (it was `…_hides_the_view_s_hints`): the hints shown with Lighting open and Delete deleting the selected waypoint [`any_open` back in `keys_blocked`: "the hints under the popover: ["1 selected", "4 by 4 tiles", "2 objects"]"].
- **A2. The Overlays switch is kept** as Minimap and Sound ranges are: `Settings::hide_overlays` (serde's default: a file from before reads it on, an older Moonglow ignores it), written as the switch changes, read by a view opened after. **B3.** While it is off, the Overlays item stays on the header even with all four of its rows hidden (the one way to turn it on again). `the_overlays_switch_is_kept_and_stays_reachable` [the settings not written: "kept in the settings"; the item left out with no rows: "Overlays kept while off: [… no Overlays …]"].
- **B1. Bake Walkmesh to Transform** turns the switch on with Object walkmeshes. The menu runs while the view is out of `app.area_views` (it is drawn), so `bake_view::open`'s `area_views.get_mut` found nothing: from that menu, Object walkmeshes were not turned on either. `menu_items` now takes the view and sets both on it; `open` keeps the setting. `bake_walkmesh_is_offered_with_bake_rules_and_a_transform` opens it with the switch off [the view's line taken out: "the objects' walkmeshes drawn"].
- **B2. Wide-row coverage.** `common::header_holds` checks an unfolded popover's every part by name (Shadows, Fade Geometry, Walkmesh, each row) against the row's edges, More and the end slot, and every part checked clear of every other (a part measured short overlaps the next, inside the row); `the_area_header_holds_every_control_from_420_to_1600` sweeps on every 40 points to 3000, where the popovers unfold and fold. With the reviewer's break (Shadows measured 30 short) it fails ["Shaders and Overlays are drawn over each other on Area header's row: [[1314.0 72.5] - [1336.0 94.5]] [[1310.0 72.5] - [1392.0 94.5]]"]: the slack no longer hides it.
- **C1.** No separator after Day | Night or the moon (neither board has one). **C2.** Together and Onto Objects one joined group, "Placing" (`Item::joined`; its parts keep their names, and Yields' 490 sends the group into More). **C3.** The compact Show's count in `text-3` at 11 points after its words (`.count-w`): a new `KitButton::count` and `Item::count` (add-only), `COUNT_GAP`; `an_item_s_count_follows_its_words` (kit). **C4.** `board_wide` sets the split's share so the area is WideArea's 1729 of the two groups' 2370 points: Lighting, Overlays and Audio are unfolded as the board draws them. For it, the popovers' separators are shown hung only (`Popover::folded_only` after them; the board's wide row has none inside a popover), and **the popovers unfold only in the wide layout** (a window from 2560, `Yields::wide`): with the separators gone the Main board's row had room to unfold Audio at 1600, which the board keeps a button. **C5.** Overlays' and Audio's tips are Main's: "Grid, minimap, walkmeshes and sound ranges", "Placed sounds, ambient sound and music".
- **D1.** Manual 04: "Sound ranges". **D2.** The Options tip, one short sentence: "The editors' toolbar buttons with words or as icons alone, not the area's header".

Differences from WideArea left: no lines between the unfolded popovers' groups (a line between popover items would stand between their buttons on the Main board too); the Overlays switch stands in the wide row as an icon toggle (U3's decision 3). Pictures in `target/ui-mock/impl/m4-u3-fix/` (board_main, board_narrow, board_wide, `area_header_narrow`'s rows and popovers with the compact Show, Overlays and Audio, `area_shadows_menu` at 1200), Dark and Light, compared with Main, Narrow and WideArea.

**U4. The tool shelf, the compass and Paint (2 days).**

- **Builds.** The shelf at the view's left (Select Objects, Select Tiles F10, Paint B, a separator, Terrain; Part 2, 8); the compass at the view's top right with Reorient Camera (now a command, `Id::ReorientCamera`, so a key and Quick Open) and Go to Start Location under it; the minimap at the bottom right. Paint as the AreaPaint board: Scatter | Row | Fill | Erase, Radius and Density in the header's left group, a **Brush** popover (Count All, Spacing, By Size, Flow, Turn, Scale, Height, Lock; Gap in Row and Fill with the note on which options each mode has; "Placing, as with the Select tool" with Snap and Turn), Together and Onto Objects back with Select; the palette's **Brush** section ("2 chosen", a row per blueprint with ×, the note).
- **Ticks.** Main Toolbar "Start" (its compass part); Area Editor "Select Tiles (F10), Paint (B)", "Paint's second strip", "Reorient Camera, Go to Start Location"; Area Editor, Paint: "Paint toggle (B)", "Scatter, Row, Fill, Erase", "Radius, Density", "Count All, Spacing…", "Gap (Row and Fill only)", "\"N chosen\" and its tip", "Snap, Turn", "Together, Onto Objects", and its three New bullets; Window at 1280: "shelf, compass".
- **Hints.** Paint's (T12) unchanged; the shelf's tips name the keys.
- **Tests.** paint_mode's strip tests follow its controls into the header and popover; new: one tool on at a time; F10 and B turn the shelf's buttons; Terrain on with a terrain brush; × takes a blueprint out of the brush; the compass's buttons run their commands; Reorient by its key.
- **Pictures.** `board_area_paint`, `board_main` (the compass and the shelf).
- **Timing.** `frame_perf` (Paint views).
- **Docs.** 04-areas (Paint, the camera), 02. Deferred: keeps "A drag when a key changes the mode" (518), "Alt + drag in Paint…" (522) and "A brush's right click is the tile menu" (1863): the shelf keeps today's rules.

**Done, 2026-11-15.** **Built.** **The tool shelf** (`area_view/tools.rs`, `shelf`): the kit's `ToolShelf` down the area body's left (under the header and the claims banner, the view to its right), named **"Area tools"** (the board's "Tools" is the Top Bar's Tools menu's name too, which tests find by label), with **Select Objects**, **Select Tiles**, **Paint**, a separator and **Terrain**; their tips name their keys ("…(F10)", "…(B)"), and in a short view the kit's More Tools holds those that do not fit. **One tool on at a time:** `tools::tool_of` says which is in hand (Paint, else Select Tiles, else a tile brush, else Select Objects) and every way of taking one goes through `tools::choose`/`toggle`: the shelf, B and F10 over the view (F10 moved from `camera_input`, which had no `app`, to `input` right after it), the tiles palette's brushes and its own Select Tiles, and the Tile window's Paint with It (`app.paint = false` with a brush). Paint puts the tile brush and Select Tiles down, Select Tiles Paint and the brush, a brush Paint and Select Tiles; a tool's button clicked again puts it down (Select Objects again). In a read-only area Select Tiles, Paint and Terrain are grayed and `choose` takes none of them. **Terrain** (decision 8, as built): there is no Terrain mode; the button is on while one of the tiles palette's brushes is in hand (`terrain_mode::active`: a terrain, crosser, group, tile, the Eraser, Raise/Lower or a tile tool), so choosing one turns it on; a click shows the Palette on its Tiles type (the type kept for the next start) and puts Paint and Select Tiles down, with no brush in hand until one is chosen (Select Objects stays lit); a click while it is on puts the brush down. **The compass** (`tools::corner`): the kit's `overlay` module (new, add-only) paints it (`paint_compass`, the light board's `cp-*` tokens in both themes: the ring `overlay` with a `line-soft` edge, the ticks `text-3`, north `error`, south `text-3`, the N `text`, turned with the needle) at the view's top right, 8 in, with the view buttons **Reorient Camera** and **Go to Start Location** under it (`ViewButton`, `.vp-btn`: 24 points, `overlay`, `raised` under the pointer, an `accent` edge with the focus; `corner_column` places them), in a group named **"Compass"**; the needle is the camera's heading (`north_of`, unit-tested against the projection looking down). Their rectangle (`AreaView::corner_rect`, kept from the frame before as the map's is, since what follows the pointer is worked out before they are drawn) is left out of what follows the pointer, as the minimap's is. **Reorient Camera is a command**, `Id::ReorientCamera` with `keys::Cmd::ReorientCamera` (General, **Ctrl+Alt+R**: no Aurora key; free in every group, not a bare letter since it acts wherever the pointer is; it gives way to a key a builder gave another command, as Quick Open does, `GIVES_WAY`), in `Id::all()` (Quick Open, Options › Keyboard; no menu), acting on the area shown last (`shown_area`, enabled only with one). **The minimap** is at the view's bottom right, 10 in, and smaller (or left out, under 40 points of room) where it would meet the compass's column. **Paint as the AreaPaint board** (`paint_mode::header_items`): while Paint is the tool in hand the header's left group is the mode as a segmented control (**Scatter | Row | Fill | Erase**, the group "How the brush paints"), **Radius** and **Density** number fields where the mode takes them (today's rule: Scatter both, Erase the radius alone, Row and Fill neither), and the **Brush** popover (`Popover::min_width(286)`, its title's note the mode, `Popover::title_note`): Count all, Spacing with By size, Flow, Turn, Scale from and to (Scatter), Gap (Row and Fill), Height and Lock (all but Erase), the board's note "Row and Fill take Gap and Height, Erase the radius alone.", a line, "Placing, as with the Select tool" and the Select tool's **Snap** and **Turn** combo boxes (which stay open over the popover, the kit's rule). Each has a form for More, where it goes as the row narrows. The Show toggles, Snap, Turn, Together and Onto Objects leave the row while Paint is on and come back with Select Objects; `paint_mode::strip` is gone. The settings are written back after the header (`Cell` of `PaintSettings`). **The palette's Brush section** (`paint_mode::brush_section`, drawn by `palette_view` in a bottom panel under the placeables' list while Paint is on): "Brush" as a section heading with "2 chosen" (or "none chosen") in `text-3`, a rule, a row per blueprint (the placeable's icon, its name from the palette, its ResRef) with × (`take_out`: one chosen with Ctrl + click goes, the one clicked is replaced by the next chosen), and the note, or "Choose placeables in the palette (Ctrl + click for several)." with none chosen; its group "Brush blueprints". **Decided:** (1) `hidden_area_tools` keeps its 29 names: "Select Tiles" and "Paint" hide those shelf buttons, "Reorient Camera" and "Go to Start Location" the compass's (their keys and View › Go to Start Location stay); the header's right-click menu and Options › Area › Header still list them; Select Objects and Terrain have no name and are always shown. (2) While Paint is on, the Show toggles are the Show popover in More, after Paint's options (the board leaves them to the Outliner's eyes, but the Outliner has no Start eye, nor All and None: nothing is lost). (3) The Brush popover's fields are named by their words for a screen reader (`accesskit` label; the words are painted, not a label of their own), so a test or a screen reader finds the field by "Gap"; the Turn spread field is named **"Turn spread"** apart from the Turn combo box beside it in the same popover, and the scale's second field **"Scale to"**. (4) Paint's options in Select Tiles: the left group stays the Select tool's until U5's Tiles state. **Renamed (accessible names):** "Count All" is **"Count all"**, "By Size" **"By size"** (the board's sentence case); the strip's unnamed number fields are named "Radius", "Density", "Spacing", "Flow", "Turn spread", "Scale", "Scale to", "Gap", "Height" (their words were labels beside them); "2 chosen" is in the section, not the strip. Kept: "Paint", "Select Tiles", "Reorient Camera", "Go to Start Location", "Scatter", "Row", "Fill", "Erase", "Lock", "Snap", "Turn". New: "Select Objects", "Terrain", "Area tools", "Compass", "Brush", "How the brush paints", "Brush blueprints", "Take RESREF out of the brush (Ctrl+click it in the palette)". **The kit (add-only):** `overlay` (`COMPASS`, `VIEW_BUTTON`, `CORNER_INSET`, `CORNER_GAP`, `paint_compass`, `corner_column`, `ViewButton`), `Popover::title_note`, `Popover::min_width`; `mg-uikit`'s crate comment names the overlay module. **Differences from the boards left:** the number fields' values stand in their middle, not at their right; the dark compass takes the light board's token roles (its needle's north `#f0666a`, the dark `error`, where the Main board has `#e5484d`; its south `text-3`, the board `text-2`); Day | Night has no sun and moon (U3's); the minimap keeps today's size (up to 240 points, a third of the view's least side) where the board draws 132; Brush rows 24 points apart, the board 28. **Tests.** New, each seen to fail with its behavior broken (the task's report lists the breaks and their failure text; `target/u4/breaks/`): `area_tools.rs` (in the `app` binary): `one_tool_is_on_at_a_time` (docked and floating), `f10_and_b_turn_the_shelf_s_buttons` (and their tips' keys), `the_shelf_and_the_compass_are_reached_by_tab`, `terrain_is_on_with_a_tile_brush_in_hand` (and a brush chosen in the tiles palette itself with Paint on puts Paint down), `hidden_names_leave_the_shelf_and_the_compass` (and no Placing heading over nothing with Snap and Turn hidden), `x_takes_a_blueprint_out_of_the_brush`, `the_compass_s_buttons_run_their_commands` (nothing placed by a click on them with Paint on or a blueprint in hand), `reorient_by_its_key_and_quick_open`, `the_minimap_stands_at_the_bottom_right`; `area_header::paint_s_options_are_held_at_every_width_in_each_mode` (`header_holds` from 420 to 1600 and every 40 to 3000 in each of the four modes, both modes of Open editors, and every Brush row, Show Start, All and None found at 420, 1600 and 3000); the unit test `area_view::tools::tests::the_needle_points_where_north_is_drawn`; the kit's `overlay.rs` (`a_popover_is_as_wide_as_asked_with_its_title_s_note`, `the_corner_column_stands_at_the_view_s_top_right`, `a_view_button_is_named_and_drawn_in_the_tokens`, both themes). Migrated: the paint tests (`area_viewer_lays_a_row_of_placeables`, `…fills_a_rectangle…`, `…erases_locks_and_goes_on_from_a_row`) find Gap, Fill, Lock and Erase through `area_tool`/`area_click`; `common::area_tool` knows the shelf, its More Tools, the compass and the Brush popover (`AREA_HOLDERS`), `in_toolbar` the same; `header_holds` scrolls a group in a long More by its first part; `area_header`'s `held_names` lost the four moved names and knows Paint's; `what_follows_the_pointer_stops_under_a_window` takes Select Tiles before the floating window opens (a window held to the screen's height is moved up over the shelf, and an accessibility click reaches nothing under a window with Cancel). **Pictures:** `board_area_paint`, `board_main`, and the new `area_paint_brush` (the Brush popover open in Scatter, Row and Erase, Snap's list open over it, none chosen, and the header at 900 with Paint's options in More) and `area_compass` (the boards' camera, turned 135°, reoriented), Dark and Light, in `target/ui-mock/impl/m4-u4/` with the before pictures. **Timing:** `frame_perf` release, a Paint view added to every area's views in both trees ("Paint on, two placeables chosen": the palette shown, the brush's circle and next dab following the pointer; the same lines patched into the tip's copy), each run alone at 1-minute loads of 2.4 to 2.9 at its start, the tip (`1b86eb56`, a `git archive` copy built apart) and U4's tree interleaved, tip, U4, U4, tip (`target/ui-mock/perf-m4/u4/`, `agree.txt`, `compare.py`): of 363 views the median difference is +0.010 ms in both runs (median view 1.670 against 1.690 ms), tessellation 43.4 against 44.1 and 43.7 ms summed; the Paint views within the tip's own spread (area084's 2.5 to 5.5 ms, the rest under 3.6 ms), some 1,460 vertices fewer (no strip). Moved over 0.3 ms in both runs: eleven area084 and one area092 wheel views 0.3 to 2.5 ms faster (all after the new Paint view in the run; not explained: the wheel still turns over the view, not the shelf, and the two trees' state after the Paint view is the same), and area084's "all selected, walkmesh" under the wheel 0.3 and 0.7 ms slower (5.45 against 6.18 and 5.76); the two tip runs alone differ by over 0.3 ms in 25 views; over the 6.94 ms budget only the four area084 wheel views over it before (overview and turning). **Not built, by the plan:** the Tiles state's left group (U5); the Properties panel (U6). No deferred entry settled or added (230 before and after); kept, as the plan says: "A drag when a key changes the mode", "Alt + drag in Paint…" and "A brush's right click is the tile menu" (the shelf keeps today's rules: a tool changed lets go of what was in hand, `view.let_go`). **The next tasks inherit:** `tools::choose` for every way of taking a tool (U5's Tiles state reads `tools::tool_of`); the header's left group by tool (`header::header` branches on `painting`; U5 adds Select Tiles' items the same way); the kit's `overlay` for the readout chip (U8) and the view's corner buttons elsewhere; `common::area_tool` finding the shelf's, the compass's and the Brush popover's controls.

#### Rulings for M4

Five choices the boards, the proposal and the earlier rulings leave open, each with a recommendation and the task it gates. A task before its gate does not depend on the answer.

- **Q1. A double click on a placed object.** Today it opens the object's Properties as a tab or window with OK and Cancel (area_view.rs:5553-5559). The Main and Floating boards draw the Properties panel in both modes, but ruling 1 of the second set promises an existing builder no window changed by an update. (a) A double click shows the Properties panel and brings it to the front, unfolding the Right Dock, in both modes; Open in Tab and the context menu's Properties… open today's tab or window. (b) As (a) As Tabs, but As Floating Windows a double click keeps opening the window. **Recommended: (a).** The panel applies at once as the window already does (its Cancel is one Undo step), the window stays one click away, and ruling 1 is about where editors open, not where a placed object's fields are. Gates U6. **Ruled by the owner, 2026-11-14: (a), as recommended.**
- **Q2. The module tree's area rows (R2's remainder).** The boards' Module panel lists areas without arrows. (a) Keep them, as T13's ruling left the tree (it keeps an area opened out when the builder opens it, and follows the selection only while no Outliner shows). (b) Remove them: the Outliner shows the area in front. **Recommended: (a)**: the objects of an area not in front are reachable only there, nothing disappears, and the boards simply show no area opened. Gates U6. **Ruled by the owner, 2026-11-14: (a), as recommended.**
- **Q3. Plugin panels (R3, to be revisited "when the dialogs become panels in M4").** (a) Tabs or windows, following the Open editors switch, as R3 built them. (b) Right Dock panels. **Recommended: (a)**: they are no one editor's, and the Right Dock follows the active editor, so a plugin's panel would come and go with the tab in front. Gates U29. **Ruled by the owner, 2026-11-14: (a), as recommended.**
- **Q4. Build Module, between the dialog and Problems.** Ruling 5 sends its results to Problems; the Dialogs board draws Build Module whole as a dialog. (a) The dialog keeps its options and Build, Mark as Used, Export… and Done; its Results and Counted as Used move to Problems, which Build opens. (b) The options become a Bottom Dock panel beside Problems too. (c) As the board: results stay in the dialog. **Recommended: (a)**: it keeps the board's dialog and the ruling's Problems, and results are what is worked through after the dialog is closed. Gates U28. **Ruled by the owner, 2026-11-14: (a), as recommended.**
- **Q5. Verify's findings in Problems.** Today Verify writes its findings to the log, with fixes beside them (09-build-verify-test, "Verify Module"). (a) They also go to Problems, with their fixes, the log keeping its lines. (b) Problems holds Build Module's results and the tileset's Check alone. **Recommended: (a)**: the Problems switch's badge reads as the module's problems, and Verify's are the main ones; but section 14 lists no such feature, so it is the owner's. Gates U28. **Ruled by the owner, 2026-11-14: (a), as recommended.**

|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Proposal 9.7: the talk table's Right Dock is String and Talk Table | Talk.png also draws a References panel (the line's users) | The board: U17 builds three |

| 6 | The order paragraph puts the dialogs that become panels last | The script (Search Results) and the tileset (Problems, proposal 9.9) need those panels before | U2 builds the mechanism, U13 and U20 the first feeders, U28 the dialogs |

In this order, each its own change (the tasks U0 to U30 above): the area editor (header, popovers, tool shelf, docks, Paint and Tiles states), the gizmos (section 11), blueprints (page list, columns, Preview panel), the script editor, the conversation editor, the tables (2DA, talk table), the rest (NUI, tileset, hak, resources, journal, factions, Options, Welcome), the galleries and pickers (the shared cell), and the dialogs that become panels. The checklist is [ui-redesign-moves.md](ui-redesign-moves.md); a row is ticked when its control has reached its home and a test (or a picture compared with the board) says so.

### M5. The manual, the parity inventory and a toolset release

- The manual's chapters rewritten where the screens changed, the screenshots replaced, the parity inventory (`docs/parity/aurora-ui-inventory.md`) brought up to date.
- A toolset release, cut as this repository's releases are (an annotated tag, hand-written notes; **the owner signs each release**).
- Exit: `cargo test` suites and clippy clean on the newest stable; the corpus tests pass; frame time recorded against the baseline.

### M6. The viewer

- The viewer moves to the toolset release's tag and takes `mg-uikit`, the shell and the look; the copied helpers go.
- Its renames (Nodes to Outliner, Animation to Timeline, the View menu names) and Gallery mode for Resources, with **its manual updated in the same change**.
- Exit: the viewer's boards (Model with ASCII Editor, Creature and Colors, 3440 by 1440, Gallery) reproduced in pictures; frame time measured.

## 17. Open for the owner

Nothing in the design is open. Two things are the owner's by custom: ruling 7 (Ubuntu Light) is judged after M2, and each release is signed by the owner (M5, M6).
